===== src/hardening.js =====
// hardening.js — drop-in fixes for Eat & Park POS V15 (React + Firestore)
// Import what you need into your main file and delete the old versions.
import { doc, runTransaction } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "./firebase";

/* ───────────── 1. Input / data normalisation ───────────── */
export const MAX_QTY = 99;
export const TABLE_COUNT = 12; // use everywhere instead of hardcoded 12

export const num = (v, d = 0) => { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : d; };
export const clampQty = (q) => Math.min(MAX_QTY, Math.max(0, Math.round(num(q) * 100) / 100));

// FIX: phone was used raw as a Firestore doc id. "123/456" made doc() throw
// synchronously inside useEffect and crashed the whole customer screen.
export const cleanPhone = (p) => String(p || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
export const isValidPhone = (p) => /^[6-9]\d{9}$/.test(p);

// FIX: createdAt could be missing / Timestamp / string -> toISOString() threw RangeError
// and one bad doc blanked the Admin dashboard.
export const toMillis = (v) => {
  if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
  if (v && typeof v.toMillis === "function") return v.toMillis();
  const n = Date.parse(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
};
export const toLocalISODate = (ts) => {
  const t = toMillis(ts);
  if (!t) return "";
  const d = new Date(t);
  return new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000).toISOString().split("T")[0];
};
export const normOrder = (d) => {
  const raw = d.data() || {};
  return {
    ...raw,
    id: d.id,
    createdAt: toMillis(raw.createdAt),
    items: (Array.isArray(raw.items) ? raw.items : []).map((it) => ({
      ...it, price: num(it.price), qty: num(it.qty),
    })),
    customer: raw.customer || { name: "", phone: "" },
  };
};

// FIX: CSV formula injection (=cmd|...) when opened in Excel
export const csvCell = (c) => {
  const s = String(c ?? "");
  return /^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(s) ? "'" + s : s;
};

/* ───────────── 2. Business day (orders vanished at midnight) ───────────── */
// FIX: query used midnight, so a served-but-unpaid table ("billing") dropped off the
// board at 00:00 while the app only reloads at 05:00. Use the same 5am cutoff.
export function businessDayStart(now = new Date(), startHour = 5) {
  const d = new Date(now);
  d.setHours(startHour, 0, 0, 0);
  if (d > now) d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  return d.getTime();
}

/* ───────────── 3. Orders: atomic, idempotent, stale-safe ───────────── */
// FIX: old version did getDoc() then setDoc() and swallowed getDoc errors. After a
// timed-out write that actually landed, a retry overwrote an order the kitchen had
// already advanced. Transaction = create only if it doesn't exist.
export async function setOrderOnce(order) {
  const ref = doc(db, "orders", order.id);
  await runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    if (!snap.exists()) tx.set(ref, order);
  });
}

// FIX: advanceStatus trusted the *clicked* status. A stale tablet could push an order
// from "ready" back to "preparing". Now it aborts if the server status moved on.
export async function advanceStatusSafe(orderId, expected, STATUS_FLOW) {
  const i = STATUS_FLOW.indexOf(expected);
  if (i < 0) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid" };
  const next = STATUS_FLOW[Math.min(i + 1, STATUS_FLOW.length - 1)];
  if (next === expected) return { ok: false, reason: "final" };
  const ref = doc(db, "orders", orderId);
  return runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    if (!snap.exists()) return { ok: false, reason: "missing" };
    const cur = snap.data();
    if (cur.status === "cancelled") return { ok: false, reason: "cancelled" };
    if (cur.status !== expected) return { ok: false, reason: "stale", current: cur.status };
    const patch = { status: next };
    if (next === "served") patch.servedAt = Date.now();
    if (cur.kots?.length) patch.kots = cur.kots.map((k) => (k.status === expected ? { ...k, status: next } : k));
    tx.update(ref, patch);
    return { ok: true, next };
  });
}
// Caller: remove the optimistic setOrdersState, or roll it back when ok === false
// (the snapshot will NOT re-fire if the server doc didn't change).

// FIX: item-count comparison missed price/qty edits (lost updates between two devices).
// Use a revision counter bumped ONLY by item-affecting writers (bill save, running KOT).
export async function guardedOrderUpdate(base, patch) {
  const ref = doc(db, "orders", base.id);
  await runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    if (!snap.exists()) throw new Error("Order nahi mila");
    const cur = snap.data();
    if ((cur.rev || 0) !== (base.rev || 0)) {
      throw new Error("Ye order kisi aur ne abhi badla hai. Bill band karke dobara kholo.");
    }
    tx.update(ref, { ...patch, rev: (cur.rev || 0) + 1 });
  });
}
// Also add `rev: (cur.rev||0)+1` to addRunningItems' tx.update.

// FIX: customer cancel was only hidden in the UI; kitchen could already be cooking.
export async function cancelOrderIfNew(orderId, by = "customer") {
  const ref = doc(db, "orders", orderId);
  await runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    if (!snap.exists()) throw new Error("Order not found");
    if (snap.data().status !== "new") throw new Error("Kitchen ne order shuru kar diya hai — staff se cancel karwao.");
    tx.update(ref, { status: "cancelled", cancelledAt: Date.now(), cancelledBy: by });
  });
}

/* ───────────── 4. Loyalty: ledger inside the transaction ───────────── */
// FIX: coinHistory was written AFTER the transaction from stale local state:
//  - ledger could differ from the balance actually applied
//  - double tap = duplicate history rows
// Deterministic doc ids make this idempotent.
export async function markPaidSafe(orderId, paid, loyaltyRate, getOrderTotal) {
  await runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
    const oref = doc(db, "orders", orderId);
    const osnap = await tx.get(oref);
    if (!osnap.exists()) throw new Error("Order not found");
    const o = osnap.data();
    if (o.status === "cancelled") throw new Error("Order cancelled hai");

    if (!paid) { tx.update(oref, { paid: false }); return; }
    if (o.coinsClaimed) { tx.update(oref, { paid: true }); return; }

    const phone = cleanPhone(o.customer?.phone);
    const earned = Math.floor(getOrderTotal(o) / (Number(loyaltyRate) || 10));
    const used = Math.max(0, num(o.rewardUsedCoins));

    let usnap = null;
    const uref = isValidPhone(phone) ? doc(db, "loyaltyUsers", phone) : null;
    if (uref && (earned || used)) usnap = await tx.get(uref); // all reads before writes

    if (uref && usnap) {
      const next = Math.max(0, num(usnap.exists() ? usnap.data().coins : 0) + earned - used);
      if (usnap.exists()) tx.update(uref, { coins: next });
      else tx.set(uref, { phone, name: o.customer?.name || "Guest", coins: next });
      const ts = Date.now();
      const tag = orderId.slice(1, 5).toUpperCase();
      if (earned > 0) tx.set(doc(db, "coinHistory", orderId + "_earn"), { phone, coins: earned, reason: `Order #${tag}`, timestamp: ts });
      if (used > 0) tx.set(doc(db, "coinHistory", orderId + "_use"), { phone, coins: -used, reason: `Redeemed #${tag}`, timestamp: ts });
    }
    tx.update(oref, { paid: true, coinsClaimed: true });
  });
}

/* ───────────── 5. Menu: no more whole-menu overwrite from stale state ───────────── */
// FIX: every add/edit wrote the full `items` array from the caller's local copy, so two
// devices (admin + waiter) silently erased each other's changes, and a swallowed error
// still showed "✅ Dish added". Also refuses to save an empty menu.
// Strip the runtime-only `available` flag (menuEff adds it) before saving.
export async function updateMenuAtomic(mutator) {
  const ref = doc(db, "settings", "menu");
  return runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    const cur = snap.exists() && Array.isArray(snap.data().items) ? snap.data().items : [];
    const next = mutator(cur);
    if (!Array.isArray(next) || next.length === 0) throw new Error("Refusing to save an empty menu");
    tx.set(ref, { items: next });
    return next;
  });
}
// Usage: await updateMenuAtomic((items) => [newDish, ...items]);
// Long term: one doc per dish in a `menu` collection (1 MB doc limit, no write races).

/* ───────────── 6. Razorpay: promise that can never hang ───────────── */
// FIX: `new Promise(async ...)` swallowed any throw (e.g. new Razorpay() failing) and the
// promise never settled -> isProcessingPayment stuck forever. Script was also appended
// again on every call while the first load was pending.
let rzpLoader = null;
export function loadRazorpay() {
  if (window.Razorpay) return Promise.resolve(true);
  if (!rzpLoader) {
    rzpLoader = new Promise((resolve) => {
      const s = document.createElement("script");
      s.src = "https://checkout.razorpay.com/v1/checkout.js";
      s.onload = () => resolve(true);
      s.onerror = () => { rzpLoader = null; resolve(false); };
      document.body.appendChild(s);
    });
  }
  return rzpLoader;
}
export async function payWithRazorpay({ key, amount, orderId, name, desc, prefill, color }) {
  if (!key || key.startsWith("YOUR_")) throw new Error("Razorpay key configure nahi hai");
  if (!(await loadRazorpay()) || !window.Razorpay) throw new Error("Payment gateway load nahi hua");
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    try {
      const rzp = new window.Razorpay({
        key, amount: Math.round(amount * 100), currency: "INR", name,
        description: desc, order_id: undefined, // TODO: create order server-side and pass order_id
        prefill, theme: { color },
        handler: resolve,
        modal: { ondismiss: () => resolve(null) },
      });
      rzp.on("payment.failed", () => resolve(null));
      rzp.open();
    } catch (e) { reject(e); }
  });
}
// NOTE: the client can never prove a payment happened. Verify razorpay_signature in a
// Cloud Function / API route before setting paid=true. Today it is "paid_unverified".

/* ───────────── 7. AI order: validate everything the model returns ───────────── */
// FIX: BillPOS.applyAi did `next[i].qty + line.qty` -> string concat if qty came as "2";
// WaiterOrderPanel could push NaN into the cart; CustomerView accepted qty = 1e9.
export function sanitizeAiLines(data, menu) {
  const byId = new Map((menu || []).map((m) => [m.id, m]));
  const items = [];
  for (const l of Array.isArray(data?.items) ? data.items : []) {
    const m = byId.get(l?.id);
    const qty = Math.round(num(l?.qty));
    if (!m || !m.available || qty < 1) continue;
    items.push({ id: m.id, qty: Math.min(qty, MAX_QTY) });
  }
  const t = Math.round(num(data?.table));
  return {
    items,
    orderType: data?.orderType === "parcel" ? "parcel" : "dine_in",
    table: t >= 1 && t <= TABLE_COUNT ? t : null,
    notes: String(data?.notes || "").slice(0, 200),
    unmatched: (Array.isArray(data?.unmatched) ? data.unmatched : []).slice(0, 10).map(String),
  };
}
// FIX: fetch had no timeout; a hung API froze the modal and applied the order after close.
export async function fetchWithTimeout(url, opts = {}, ms = 15000) {
  const ctl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), ms);
  try { return await fetch(url, { ...opts, signal: ctl.signal }); }
  finally { clearTimeout(t); }
}
// Also: abort in a useEffect cleanup when the modal unmounts, and stop SpeechRecognition there.

/* ───────────── 9. Dark mode without breaking position:fixed ───────────── */
// FIX: `.dark-theme { filter: invert(...) }` makes the wrapper the containing block for
// every position:fixed child (modals, cart bar, FABs scroll with the page) and is very
// heavy on phones. Use CSS variables instead:
export const THEME_CSS = `
:root{--bg:#FAFAF8;--ink:#1A1A1A;--card:#fff;--line:#E8E6DC}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--bg:#121212;--ink:#F2F2F2;--card:#1E1E1E;--line:#333}
body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink)}
`;
// toggle with document.documentElement.dataset.theme = isDark ? "dark" : "light"
===== src/recover.js =====
const RELOAD_FLAG = "ep_fs_recover_ts";
const RELOAD_COOLDOWN_MS = 60_000;
const FS_ERROR_PATTERN = /INTERNAL ASSERTION FAILED|isCorePipeline|getTargetData/i;

function isFirestoreCrash(err) {
  if (!err) return false;
  const msg = String(err.message || (err.reason && err.reason.message) || err);
  return FS_ERROR_PATTERN.test(msg);
}

async function wipeFirestoreIndexedDB() {
  if (typeof indexedDB === "undefined") return;
  try {
    const dbs = indexedDB.databases ? await indexedDB.databases() : [];
    await Promise.all(
      dbs.filter((d) => d.name && d.name.toLowerCase().includes("firestore"))
        .map((d) => new Promise((res) => {
          const r = indexedDB.deleteDatabase(d.name);
          r.onsuccess = r.onerror = r.onblocked = () => res();
        }))
    );
  } catch (e) { console.warn("[recover] IDB wipe failed:", e); }
}

export async function tryRecover(err) {
  if (!isFirestoreCrash(err)) return false;
  try {
    const last = Number(sessionStorage.getItem(RELOAD_FLAG) || 0);
    if (Date.now() - last < RELOAD_COOLDOWN_MS) return false;
    sessionStorage.setItem(RELOAD_FLAG, String(Date.now()));
  } catch (_) {}
  console.warn("[recover] Firestore crash → wipe cache + reload");
  await wipeFirestoreIndexedDB();
  window.location.reload();
  return true;
}

if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  window.addEventListener("error", (e) => tryRecover(e.error || e.message));
  window.addEventListener("unhandledrejection", (e) => tryRecover(e.reason));
}
===== src/lib/guards.js =====
// Order ka chhota signature: items, qty, KOT ya total badle to alag aayega.
// (status change ya kitchen ka Start/Ready isse nahi badalta)
export const orderSig = (o) => [
  (o.items || []).length,
  (o.items || []).reduce((s, it) => s + (Number(it.qty) || 0), 0),
  (o.kots || []).length,
  Number(o.finalTotal) || 0,
].join("|");

export function assertEditable(live, seen) {
  if (!live) throw new Error("Order ab maujood nahi hai");
  if (live.paid) throw new Error("Order paid ho chuka hai - edit nahi ho sakta");
  if (live.status === "cancelled") throw new Error("Order cancelled hai");
  if (seen && orderSig(live) !== orderSig(seen)) throw new Error("Order kisi aur ne badal diya - wapas table kholo");
}

export const resolveVendorId = (name, map) =>
  map[String(name || "").trim().toLowerCase()] || null;
===== src/lib/guards.test.js =====
import { describe, test, expect } from "vitest";
import { assertEditable, resolveVendorId } from "./guards";

describe("assertEditable", () => {
  test("rejects missing order", () => expect(() => assertEditable(null, { items: [] })).toThrow(/maujood/));
  test("rejects paid", () => expect(() => assertEditable({ paid: true, items: [] }, { items: [] })).toThrow(/paid/));
  test("rejects cancelled", () => expect(() => assertEditable({ status: "cancelled", items: [] }, { items: [] })).toThrow(/cancelled/));
  test("allows unchanged open order", () =>
    expect(() => assertEditable({ items: [{ itemId: "a", qty: 1 }] }, { items: [{ itemId: "a", qty: 1 }] })).not.toThrow());
});

describe("resolveVendorId", () => {
  test("case + whitespace insensitive", () => expect(resolveVendorId("  Ramesh ", { ramesh: "v1" })).toBe("v1"));
  test("unknown vendor -> null", () => expect(resolveVendorId("Suresh", { ramesh: "v1" })).toBeNull());
  test("empty name -> null", () => expect(resolveVendorId("", { ramesh: "v1" })).toBeNull());
});
===== src/lib/orderRules.test.js =====
// Chalane ke liye:  node --test src/lib/orderRules.test.js
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {
  isActiveOrder, isOpenDineIn, isOpenParcel, isDue, paidOrders, paidRevenue, buildPaidPatch, OPEN_WINDOW_MS,
} from "./orderRules.js";

const now = () => Date.now();
const mk = (o) => ({ orderType: "dine_in", status: "new", paid: false, createdAt: now(), finalTotal: 100, ...o });

test("unpaid order active hai", () => {
  assert.equal(isActiveOrder(mk({})), true);
});
test("served par bhi unpaid hai to active hai", () => {
  assert.equal(isActiveOrder(mk({ status: "served" })), true);
});
test("paid order active nahi, chahe status ready ho", () => {
  assert.equal(isActiveOrder(mk({ paid: true, status: "ready" })), false);
});
test("due order active nahi", () => {
  assert.equal(isActiveOrder(mk({ paymentStatus: "due" })), false);
});
test("cancelled order active nahi", () => {
  assert.equal(isActiveOrder(mk({ status: "cancelled" })), false);
});

test("chalu dine-in table occupied hai", () => {
  assert.equal(isOpenDineIn(mk({})), true);
});
test("paid dine-in table occupied nahi", () => {
  assert.equal(isOpenDineIn(mk({ paid: true })), false);
});
test("18 ghante se purana order table occupied nahi", () => {
  assert.equal(isOpenDineIn(mk({ createdAt: now() - OPEN_WINDOW_MS - 1000 })), false);
});
test("parcel dine-in nahi ginta, aur dine-in parcel nahi", () => {
  assert.equal(isOpenDineIn(mk({ orderType: "parcel" })), false);
  assert.equal(isOpenParcel(mk({ orderType: "dine_in" })), false);
  assert.equal(isOpenParcel(mk({ orderType: "parcel" })), true);
});
test(".filter(isOpenDineIn) index ki wajah se galat nahi hota", () => {
  const list = [mk({}), mk({}), mk({ paid: true })];
  assert.equal(list.filter(isOpenDineIn).length, 2);
});

test("isDue sirf unpaid due ko pakadta hai", () => {
  assert.equal(isDue(mk({ paymentStatus: "due" })), true);
  assert.equal(isDue(mk({ paymentStatus: "due", paid: true })), false);
});

test("revenue me sirf paid aur non-cancelled", () => {
  const list = [
    mk({ paid: true, finalTotal: 500 }),
    mk({ paid: true, finalTotal: 300, status: "cancelled" }),
    mk({ paid: false, finalTotal: 900 }),
    mk({ paid: true, finalTotal: "200" }),
  ];
  assert.equal(paidOrders(list).length, 2);
  assert.equal(paidRevenue(list), 700);
});
test("finalTotal na ho to 0 maana jaye", () => {
  assert.equal(paidRevenue([mk({ paid: true, finalTotal: undefined })]), 0);
});

test("buildPaidPatch: sahi fields likhta hai", () => {
  const p = buildPaidPatch(mk({}), "upi", "Ramesh");
  assert.equal(p.paid, true);
  assert.equal(p.paymentStatus, "paid");
  assert.equal(p.payment, "upi");
  assert.equal(p.paidBy, "Ramesh");
  assert.equal(typeof p.paidAt, "number");
});
test("buildPaidPatch: naam na ho to Staff", () => {
  assert.equal(buildPaidPatch(mk({}), "cash").paidBy, "Staff");
});
test("buildPaidPatch: parcel paid hote hi served", () => {
  assert.equal(buildPaidPatch(mk({ orderType: "parcel", status: "new" }), "cash").status, "served");
});
test("buildPaidPatch: ready dine-in served ho jata hai", () => {
  assert.equal(buildPaidPatch(mk({ status: "ready" }), "cash").status, "served");
});
test("buildPaidPatch: preparing/new ka status nahi chhedta (kitchen se gayab na ho)", () => {
  assert.equal("status" in buildPaidPatch(mk({ status: "preparing" }), "cash"), false);
  assert.equal("status" in buildPaidPatch(mk({ status: "new" }), "cash"), false);
});
===== src/lib/orderRules.js =====
// Eat & Park - order ke saare business rules yahin hain.
// Rule badalna ho to sirf yahan badlo; Tables, Dashboard, Orders sab yahi use karte hain.
// NOTE: ye functions .filter(isOpenParcel) jaise seedhe pass hote hain,
// isliye inme sirf 1 argument (order) rakho. Doosra argument mat jodna.

export const OPEN_WINDOW_MS = 18 * 3600 * 1000;
export const PAID_STATUSES = ["paid"];

export const isCancelled = (o) => o.status === "cancelled";

// Due = udhaar, abhi paisa nahi aaya
export const isDue = (o) => o.paymentStatus === "due" && !o.paid && !isCancelled(o);

// Chalu order (table/parcel): unpaid, due nahi, cancelled nahi, aur 18 ghante ke andar
const isOpenBase = (o) =>
  !isCancelled(o) && !o.paid &&
  o.paymentStatus !== "due" && !PAID_STATUSES.includes(o.paymentStatus) &&
  Date.now() - (o.createdAt || 0) < OPEN_WINDOW_MS;

export const isOpenParcel = (o) => o.orderType === "parcel" && isOpenBase(o);
export const isOpenDineIn = (o) => o.orderType === "dine_in" && isOpenBase(o);

// "Active" = unpaid, due nahi, cancelled nahi (Tables, Dashboard, Orders tab teeno yahi)
export const isActiveOrder = (o) => !isCancelled(o) && !o.paid && o.paymentStatus !== "due" && Date.now() - (o.createdAt || 0) < OPEN_WINDOW_MS;

// Paid orders aur unka revenue (cancelled kabhi nahi ginte)
export const paidOrders = (orders) => orders.filter((o) => o.paid && !isCancelled(o));
export const paidRevenue = (orders) =>
  paidOrders(orders).reduce((s, o) => s + (Number(o.finalTotal) || 0), 0);

// "Paid" karte waqt Firestore me jo fields likhni hain (Orders tab + Billing dono yahi use karte hain).
// Parcel ya ready order paid hote hi "served" ho jata hai; preparing/new ka status nahi chhedte
// (warna kitchen se order beech me gayab ho jayega).
export const buildPaidPatch = (order, mode, by) => {
  const patch = {
    paid: true,
    paymentStatus: "paid",
    paidAt: Date.now(),
    paidBy: by || "Staff",
    payment: mode,
  };
  if (order.orderType === "parcel" || order.status === "ready") patch.status = "served";
  return patch;
};

function ImageUploadButton({ currentImage, onUploaded, label }) {
  const widgetRef = useRef(null);
  const onUploadedRef = useRef(onUploaded);
  onUploadedRef.current = onUploaded;
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    let tries = 0;
    const tryInit = () => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      if (window.cloudinary) {
        widgetRef.current = window.cloudinary.createUploadWidget(
          {
            cloudName: "nnddxvcn",
            uploadPreset: "eatpark_uploads",
            sources: ["local", "camera", "url"],
            multiple: false,
            maxFileSize: 5000000,
            clientAllowedFormats: ["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "webp"],
          },
          (error, result) => {
            if (error) {
              console.error("Cloudinary error:", error);
              alert("Upload fail: " + (error.statusText || error.message || JSON.stringify(error)));
              return;
            }
            if (result && result.event === "success") {
              onUploadedRef.current(result.info.secure_url);
            }
          }
        );
        setReady(true);
      } else if (++tries < 20) {
        setTimeout(tryInit, 300);
      } else {
        alert("Cloudinary script load nahi hui. public/index.html check karo.");
      }
    };
    tryInit();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, []);

  const openWidget = () => {
    if (widgetRef.current) widgetRef.current.open();
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={openWidget}
      disabled={!ready}
      style={{
        width: "100%",
        padding: 14,
        border: "none",
        borderRadius: 12,
        background: ready ? "#D97706" : "#999",
        color: "#fff",
        fontWeight: 800,
        fontSize: 14,
        cursor: ready ? "pointer" : "wait",
      }}
    >
      {ready ? (label || "📸 Upload Image") : "⏳ Loading..."}
    </button>
  );
}

const LOYALTY_TIERS = [
  { name: 'Bronze', points: 0, discount: 0.05, emoji: '🥉', color: '#CD7F32' },
  { name: 'Silver', points: 500, discount: 0.10, emoji: '🥈', color: '#C0C0C0' },
  { name: 'Gold', points: 1000, discount: 0.15, emoji: '🥇', color: '#FFD700' },
  { name: 'Platinum', points: 2000, discount: 0.25, emoji: '💎', color: '#E5E4E2' }
];

function getLoyaltyTier(points) {
  let tier = LOYALTY_TIERS[0];
  for (const t of LOYALTY_TIERS) if (points >= t.points) tier = t;
  return tier;
}

function uid(prefix) { return prefix + Date.now().toString(36).slice(-5) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6); }
function escapeHtml(s) {
  return String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c]));
}
function inr(n) {
  const num = Number(n);
  if (!Number.isFinite(num)) return "₹0";
  return "₹" + num.toLocaleString("en-IN");
}
const isWalkIn = (s) => /^walk-?in$/i.test(String(s || "").trim());
const getBaseKots = (o) => (o.kots?.length ? o.kots : [{ id: uid("kot"), kotNumber: 1, items: o.items || [], createdAt: o.createdAt, status: o.status }])
  .map(k => (k.status === "new" && o.status !== "new" ? { ...k, status: o.status } : k));
const getNextKotNo = (o) => Math.max(...getBaseKots(o).map(k => k.kotNumber || 1)) + 1;

// Ek hi jagah se order total (coupon + loyalty + delivery sab included)
function getOrderTotal(o) {
  const hasRunning = (o?.kots || []).some(k => k.isRunning);
  // If finalTotal is trusted (no running KOT or bill was manually edited), use it.
  if (Number.isFinite(o?.finalTotal) && (!hasRunning || o?.billEditedAt)) return o.finalTotal;
  // Otherwise compute from items. NOTE: items array already includes running KOT items,
  // and runningPatch() updates finalTotal only when billEditedAt is set — so no double count.
  const sub = (o?.items || []).reduce((s, it) => s + it.price * it.qty, 0);
  const coupon = Math.round((sub * (o?.discount || 0)) / 100);
  return Math.max(0, sub - coupon - (o?.loyaltyDiscount || 0)) + (o?.deliveryFee || 0);
}

// Firestore doc ko safe shape deta hai (purane/adhure docs se crash nahi hoga)
const normOrder = HX.normOrder;
const normOrderLegacy = (d) => {
  const o = { id: d.id, ...d.data() };
  if (!Array.isArray(o.items)) o.items = [];
  if (!o.customer) o.customer = { name: "", phone: "" };
  return o;
};

// Running KOT aane par total / paid status theek karta hai
const runningPatch = (o, added) => {
  const addT = added.reduce((s, it) => s + it.price * it.qty, 0);
  const p = {};
  if (o.billEditedAt) {
    const g = Number(o.gstRate) || 0;
    p.finalTotal = Math.round((Number(o.finalTotal) || 0) + addT * (1 - (Number(o.billDiscountPct) || 0) / 100) * (1 + g / 100));
    p.subtotal = (Number(o.subtotal) || 0) + addT;
  }
  if (o.paid) { p.paid = false; p.paymentStatus = "pending"; }
  return p;
};

const patchAt = (list, idx, patch) => list.map((x, i) => (i === idx ? { ...x, ...patch } : x));

// Agar bill khulne ke baad order me naya KOT/item aa gaya to save rok do
async function guardedOrderUpdate(base, patch) {
  const ref = doc(db, "orders", base.id);
  await runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    if (!snap.exists()) throw new Error("Order nahi mila");
    const cur = snap.data();
    if ((cur.items || []).length !== (base.items || []).length || (cur.kots || []).length !== (base.kots || []).length) {
      throw new Error("Ye order kisi aur ne abhi badla hai (naya KOT/item). Bill band karke dobara kholo.");
    }
    tx.update(ref, patch);
  });
}

function timeAgo(ts) { const s = Math.floor((Date.now() - ts) / 1000); if (s < 60) return s + "s ago"; const m = Math.floor(s / 60); if (m < 60) return m + "m ago"; return Math.floor(m / 60) + "h ago"; }
const toLocalISODate = HX.toLocalISODate;
function toLocalISODateLegacy(timestamp) { const d = new Date(timestamp); return new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000).toISOString().split('T')[0]; }

function getEstimatedTime(items, menu = []) {
  if (!items || items.length === 0) return 5;
  const maxTime = Math.max(...items.map(it => { 
    const item = menu.find(m => m.id === it.itemId); 
    return PREP_TIME_ESTIMATES[item?.category || "Fun Food"] || 15; 
  }));
  return (Number.isFinite(maxTime) ? maxTime : 15) + 2;
}

function getOrderProgress(status) {
  if (status === 'served' || status === 'delivered' || status === 'completed') return 100;
  if (status === 'out_for_delivery') return 90;
  if (status === 'ready') return 70;
  if (status === 'preparing') return 40;
  if (status === 'new') return 15;
  return 0;
}

// Cart helpers — Flash sale / Combo price override handle karte hain
function getCartQty(cartEntry) {
  if (!cartEntry) return 0;
  if (typeof cartEntry === 'number') return cartEntry;
  return cartEntry.qty || 0;
}

function getCartLineTotal(cartEntry, menuItem) {
  if (!cartEntry || !menuItem) return 0;
  const price = cartEntry.priceOverride ?? menuItem.price ?? 0;
  const _q = getCartQty(cartEntry);
  if (cartEntry.priceOverride == null) return price * _q;
  const _cap = Math.min(_q, cartEntry.ovQty ?? _q);
  return price * _cap + (menuItem.price ?? 0) * (_q - _cap);
}

function getSmartSuggestionPool(menu, cart) {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  let cats;
  if (hour < 11) cats = ["Shakes & Drinks", "Momos & Rolls"];
  else if (hour < 15) cats = ["Biryani & Rice", "Thali", "Dal, Roti & Chole"];
  else if (hour < 18) cats = ["Maggi, Corn & Fries", "Shakes & Drinks", "Momos & Rolls"];
  else cats = ["Tandoor", "Chinese", "Paneer & Mushroom"];
  return menu.filter(m => cats.includes(m.category) && m.available && !cart[m.id]);
}
function getSmartSuggestionPoolOld(menu, cart) {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  let pool = [];
  if (hour < 11) pool = menu.filter(m => m.category.includes("Tea") || m.category.includes("Bread"));
  else if (hour < 13) pool = menu.filter(m => m.category.includes("Biryani") || m.category.includes("Pulao"));
  else if (hour < 17) pool = menu.filter(m => m.category.includes("Snacks") || m.category.includes("Drinks"));
  else pool = menu.filter(m => m.category.includes("Tandoori") || m.category.includes("Mains"));
  return pool.filter(m => m.available && !cart[m.id]);
}

class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component {
  constructor(props) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null };
  }
  static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }
  componentDidCatch(error, info) {
    tryRecover(arguments[0]);
    console.error('🔴 ErrorBoundary caught:', error);
    console.error('🔴 Component Stack:', info.componentStack);
    this.setState({ error, errorInfo: info });
  }
  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError && this.props.compact) {
      return (
        <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, zIndex: 400, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.7)", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 16 }}>
          <div style={{ background: "#fff", borderRadius: 16, padding: 20, maxWidth: 320, textAlign: "center" }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 40 }}>⚠️</div>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, margin: "8px 0 14px" }}>Ye screen me dikkat aayi. Band karke dobara kholo.</div>
            <button onClick={() => { this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null }); if (this.props.onReset) this.props.onReset(); }} style={{ background: COLORS.ink, color: "#fff", border: "none", padding: "10px 24px", borderRadius: 10, fontWeight: 800, cursor: "pointer" }}>Theek hai</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', flexDirection: 'column', padding: 20 }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '4rem', marginBottom: '1rem' }}>😅</div>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1.5rem', fontWeight: 'bold', color: COLORS.ink, marginBottom: '0.5rem' }}>Something went wrong</h2>
          <pre style={{ background: '#f5f5f5', padding: 16, borderRadius: 8, maxWidth: 600, overflow: 'auto', fontSize: 12, color: '#c00', marginBottom: 16, whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap' }}>
            {this.state.error?.toString()}
            {'\n\n'}
            {this.state.errorInfo?.componentStack}
          </pre>
          <button onClick={() => { try { localStorage.removeItem('eatpark_cart'); } catch (e) {} window.location.reload(); }} style={{ background: COLORS.copper, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '10px 20px', borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 'bold', cursor: 'pointer' }}>Clear Cache & Refresh</button>
          <button onClick={() => this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null })} style={{ background: COLORS.ink, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '10px 20px', borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 'bold', cursor: 'pointer', marginTop: 10 }}>🔄 Try Again</button>
          <button onClick={() => this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null })} style={{ background: COLORS.ink, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '10px 20px', borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 'bold', cursor: 'pointer', marginTop: 10 }}>🔄 Try Again</button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

const VegDot = memo(({ veg }) => {
  const c = veg ? VEG : NONVEG;
  return <span role="img" aria-label={veg ? "Vegetarian" : "Non-vegetarian"} style={{ width: 14, height: 14, border: `1.5px solid ${c}`, display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", flexShrink: 0, borderRadius: 4 }}><span style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: "50%", background: c }} /></span>;
});

const Badge = memo(({ children, color }) => (

const Stepper = memo(({ qty, onChange }) => {
  const btnStyle = { width: 28, height: 28, borderRadius: "50%", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.copper}`, background: "transparent", color: COLORS.copper, fontSize: 18, lineHeight: 1, cursor: "pointer", display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", transition: "all 0.2s ease" };
  return <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 }}><button onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(0, qty - 1))} style={btnStyle}>−</button><span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontWeight: 700, minWidth: 16, textAlign: "center", fontSize: 15 }}>{qty}</span><button onClick={() => onChange(qty + 1)} style={btnStyle}>+</button></div>;

  if (!available) return <div style={{ color: COLORS.rust, background: COLORS.paper2, borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 11, padding: "6px 10px", textAlign: "center", width: 80, boxSizing: "border-box" }}>Out of stock</div>;
  if (!qty) return <button onClick={() => onChange(1)} style={{ color: COLORS.sage, background: "#fff", border: `2px solid ${COLORS.sage}`, borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 12, padding: "6px 16px", cursor: "pointer", width: 80, boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(74,124,89,0.15)" }} className="smooth-transition hover-lift">ADD</button>;
  return <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", width: 80, padding: "4px", background: "#fff", border: `2px solid ${COLORS.sage}`, borderRadius: 8 }}><button onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(0, qty - 1))} style={{ width: 22, height: 22, border: "none", color: COLORS.sage, background: "transparent", fontSize: 18, cursor: "pointer" }}>−</button><span style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 14, color: COLORS.sage }}>{qty}</span><button onClick={() => onChange(qty + 1)} style={{ width: 22, height: 22, border: "none", color: COLORS.sage, background: "transparent", fontSize: 18, cursor: "pointer" }}>+</button></div>;
});

const SearchBar = memo(({ value, onChange, placeholder = "Search menu..." }) => {
  const timeoutRef = useRef(null);
  const [localValue, setLocalValue] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => { setLocalValue(value); }, [value]);
  const handleChange = (e) => {
    const q = e.target.value;
    setLocalValue(q);
    clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    timeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => onChange(q), 250);
  };
  return (
    <div style={{ position: "relative", flex: 1 }}>
      <input type="text" value={localValue} onChange={handleChange} placeholder={placeholder} className="keep-color"
        style={{ padding: "12px 16px 12px 42px", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, width: "100%", boxSizing: "border-box", background: "#fff", color: COLORS.ink }} />
      <span style={{ position: "absolute", left: 14, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", fontSize: 18, color: COLORS.textLight }}>🔍</span>
      {localValue && (<button onClick={() => { setLocalValue(""); onChange(""); }} style={{ position: "absolute", right: 12, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", background: "none", border: "none", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 18, color: COLORS.textLight, padding: "4px 8px" }}>✕</button>)}
    </div>
  );
});

const FlashSaleItem = memo(({ item, onAdd }) => {
  if (!item.active || (item.stock != null && item.stock <= 0)) return null;
  return (
    <div style={{ background: '#fff', borderRadius: 12, border: `2px solid ${COLORS.error}`, padding: 12, minWidth: 150, flexShrink: 0 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 20 }}>🔥</div>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center', margin: '4px 0' }}>

        <span style={{ textDecoration: 'line-through', fontSize: 12, color: COLORS.textLight }}>₹{item.price}</span>
      </div>
      {item.stock != null && item.stock <= 5 && (<div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.error, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 4 }}>Only {item.stock} left!</div>)}

    </div>
  );

// 5. CUSTOM HOOKS

function useLiveNow(intervalMs = 30000) {
  const [, forceTick] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setInterval(() => forceTick(x => x + 1), intervalMs);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [intervalMs]);
  // Returns nothing on purpose — just forces a re-render every intervalMs.
  // Callers use this to refresh relative timestamps like timeAgo().
}
const useLocalStorage = (key, initialValue) => {
  const [storedValue, setStoredValue] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const item = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
      return item ? JSON.parse(item) : initialValue;
    } catch (error) { return initialValue; }
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(storedValue)); }
    catch (error) { console.error('useLocalStorage error:', error); }
  }, [key, storedValue]);
  return [storedValue, setStoredValue];
};

// 🖥️📱 APP / DESKTOP MODE
const VIEW_KEY = "eatpark_viewmode";
function useViewMode() {
  const read = () => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(VIEW_KEY);
      if (saved === "desktop" || saved === "app") return saved;
    } catch (e) {}
    return typeof window !== "undefined" && window.innerWidth >= 1024 ? "desktop" : "app";
  };
  const [mode, setModeState] = useState(read);
  useEffect(() => {
    const sync = () => setModeState(read());
    window.addEventListener("eatpark_viewmode_change", sync);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("eatpark_viewmode_change", sync);
  }, []);
  const setMode = useCallback((m) => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(VIEW_KEY, m); } catch (e) {}
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event("eatpark_viewmode_change"));
  }, []);
  return [mode, setMode];
}

// 📌 ADMIN PRESETS (Firebase: settings/presets) — waiters, customers, notes, discount, GST
const DEFAULT_PRESETS = { waiters: [], notes: [], discounts: [], gstRates: [], customers: [] };
function usePresets() {
  const [presets, setPresetsState] = useState(() => {
    try { return { ...DEFAULT_PRESETS, ...JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("eatpark_presets") || "{}") }; }
    catch (e) { return DEFAULT_PRESETS; }
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsub = onSnapshot(doc(db, "settings", "presets"), (snap) => {
      if (snap.exists()) {
        const d = { ...DEFAULT_PRESETS, ...snap.data() };
        setPresetsState(d);
        try { localStorage.setItem("eatpark_presets", JSON.stringify(d)); } catch (e) {}
      }
    }, (err) => console.error("presets listener:", err));
    return () => unsub();
  }, []);
  const savePresets = async (next) => {
    setPresetsState(next);
    try { localStorage.setItem("eatpark_presets", JSON.stringify(next)); } catch (e) {}
    try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "presets"), next); }
    catch (e) { console.error(e); alert("⚠️ Presets save nahi hue. Internet / Firebase rules check karo."); }
  };
  return [presets, savePresets];
}

const ViewModeToggle = memo(() => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useViewMode();
  const isDesktop = mode === "desktop";
  return (
    <button onClick={() => setMode(isDesktop ? "app" : "desktop")} title="App / Desktop mode badlo"
      style={{ background: COLORS.paper2, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 12, padding: "8px 12px", cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13, marginLeft: 8, color: COLORS.ink }}>
      {isDesktop ? "📱 App Mode" : "🖥️ Desktop Mode"}
    </button>
  );
});

// 5 galat PIN ke baad 30 second lock
function usePinLockout(max = 5, lockMs = 30000) {
  const [fails, setFails] = useState(0);
  const [lockedUntil, setLockedUntil] = useState(0);
  return {
    isLocked: () => Date.now() < lockedUntil,
    secondsLeft: () => Math.max(0, Math.ceil((lockedUntil - Date.now()) / 1000)),
    registerFail: () => {
      const n = fails + 1;
      if (n >= max) { setLockedUntil(Date.now() + lockMs); setFails(0); } else setFails(n);
    },
    reset: () => setFails(0)
  };
}

const useLocalStorageOld = (key, initialValue) => {
  const [storedValue, setStoredValue] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const item = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
      return item ? JSON.parse(item) : initialValue;
    } catch (error) {
      return initialValue;
    }
  });

  const setValue = useCallback((value) => {
    setStoredValue(prev => {
      try {
        const v = value instanceof Function ? value(prev) : value;
        window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(v));
        return v;
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('useLocalStorage error:', error);
        return prev;
      }
    });
  }, [key]);

  return [storedValue, setValue];
};

const notificationAudio = new Audio("https://assets.mixkit.co/active_storage/sfx/2869/2869-preview.mp3");
notificationAudio.volume = 0.5;
// Notification Android pe crash na kare
const safeNotify = (title, opts) => { try { return new Notification(title, opts); } catch (e) { console.warn("Notification:", e); } };
// localStorage corrupt ho to bhi crash na ho
const safeJSON = (key, fallback) => { try { const v = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || "null"); return v ?? fallback; } catch (e) { return fallback; } };
const safeSet = (key, val) => { try { localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(val)); } catch (e) { console.error("localStorage:", e); } };
const playNotificationSound = () => {
  try { notificationAudio.currentTime = 0; notificationAudio.play().catch(e => console.log("Sound play error:", e)); } catch (e) { console.log("Sound error:", e); }
};
// 🔓 Audio unlock on first user interaction (browser autoplay fix)
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  const unlockAudio = () => {
    notificationAudio.play().then(() => {
      notificationAudio.pause();
      notificationAudio.currentTime = 0;
    }).catch(() => {});
    document.removeEventListener('click', unlockAudio);
    document.removeEventListener('touchstart', unlockAudio);
  };
  document.addEventListener('click', unlockAudio);
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', unlockAudio);
}

function categoryImage(category) {
  const c = String(category || "");
  const hit = CATEGORY_IMG.find(([k]) => c.includes(k));
  return IMG(hit ? hit[1] : DEFAULT_IMG_ID);
}
function dishImage(name, category) {
  const n = String(name || "");
  const hit = DISH_IMG_RULES.find(([re]) => re.test(n));
  return hit ? IMG(hit[1]) : "";
}
const GENERIC_IMAGES = new Set([IMG(DEFAULT_IMG_ID), ...CATEGORY_IMG.map(([, id]) => IMG(id))]);
const withDishImage = (m) => (!m.image || GENERIC_IMAGES.has(m.image) ? { ...m, image: dishImage(m.name, m.category) } : m);

function mi(id, name, price, category, veg, desc, portion, isBestseller = false, available = true, customImg = "") {
  return { id, name, desc: desc || "Freshly prepared with premium ingredients.", price, category, veg, available, image: customImg || dishImage(name, category), portion: portion || "", isBestseller };
}

// 8. LOYALTY PROGRESS

const LoyaltyProgress = memo(({ currentPoints, nextTier, loyaltyRules }) => {
  if (!nextTier) return null;
  const currentTierIndex = LOYALTY_TIERS.indexOf(nextTier) - 1;
  const previousTierPoints = currentTierIndex >= 0 ? LOYALTY_TIERS[currentTierIndex].points : 0;
  const pointsNeeded = nextTier.points - currentPoints;
  const progress = Math.min(((currentPoints - previousTierPoints) / (nextTier.points - previousTierPoints)) * 100, 100);
  return (
    <div style={{ marginTop: 8, padding: '10px 12px', background: '#fff', borderRadius: 10, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}` }}>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 12, marginBottom: 4 }}>
        <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: COLORS.textLight }}>{nextTier.emoji} Next: {nextTier.name}</span>
        <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: pointsNeeded > 0 ? COLORS.copper : COLORS.success }}>{pointsNeeded > 0 ? `${pointsNeeded} away` : '🎉 Unlocked!'}</span>
      </div>
      <div style={{ width: '100%', height: 6, background: COLORS.paper2, borderRadius: 999, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <div style={{ width: `${Math.min(progress, 100)}%`, height: '100%', background: `linear-gradient(90deg, ${COLORS.gold}, ${nextTier.color || COLORS.sage})`, borderRadius: 999, transition: 'width 0.8s ease' }} />
      </div>
      {pointsNeeded > 0 && (<div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.textLight, marginTop: 4 }}>💰 Spend ₹{Math.ceil(pointsNeeded * loyaltyRules.rate)} more</div>)}
    </div>
  );
});

// ==
// 9. CHAT BOX
// ==

const ChatBox = memo(({ orderId, customerId }) => {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');
  const messagesRef = useMemo(() => collection(db, 'chats', orderId, 'messages'), [orderId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const q = query(messagesRef, orderBy('timestamp', 'asc'));
      const unsub = onSnapshot(q, (snap) => {
        setMessages(snap.docs.map(doc => { const data = doc.data(); if (!data.timestamp) data.timestamp = Date.now(); return { id: doc.id, ...data }; }));
      });
      return unsub;
    } catch (e) { console.error('Chat error:', e); }
  }, [orderId, messagesRef]);
  const sendMessage = async () => {
    if (!newMessage.trim()) return;
    try {
      await addDoc(messagesRef, { text: newMessage, senderId: customerId, senderName: customerId === 'customer' ? 'Customer' : 'Restaurant', timestamp: serverTimestamp() });
      setNewMessage('');
    } catch (e) { console.error('Chat send error:', e); }
  };
  return (
    <div style={{ border: '1px solid #ddd', borderRadius: 8, padding: 12, background: '#fff' }}>
      <div style={{ maxHeight: 250, overflowY: 'auto', marginBottom: 8 }}>
        {messages.map((msg) => (
          <div key={msg.id} style={{ textAlign: msg.senderId === customerId ? 'right' : 'left', margin: '4px 0' }}>
            <span style={{ background: msg.senderId === customerId ? COLORS.copper : '#e9ecef', color: msg.senderId === customerId ? '#fff' : '#000', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 12, display: 'inline-block', maxWidth: '80%' }}>{msg.text}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
        <input value={newMessage} onChange={(e) => setNewMessage(e.target.value)} placeholder="Type a message..." style={{ flex: 1, padding: 8, border: '1px solid #ccc', borderRadius: 4 }} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && sendMessage()} />
        <button onClick={sendMessage} style={{ background: COLORS.copper, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: 4, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Send</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});

  return (

      <span style={{ fontSize: size === 'lg' ? 24 : size === 'sm' ? 14 : 18 }}>⭐</span>
      {showText && <span>Rate on Google</span>}
    </a>
  );
});

// ==
// 11. PAYMENT PROCESSING
// ==

const loadRazorpayScript = () => new Promise((resolve) => {
  if (window.Razorpay) { resolve(true); return; }
  const script = document.createElement('script');
  script.src = 'https://checkout.razorpay.com/v1/checkout.js';
  script.onload = () => resolve(true);
  script.onerror = () => resolve(false);
  document.body.appendChild(script);
});

// Success pe Razorpay response, cancel/fail pe null return karta hai
const processRazorpayPaymentLegacy = (amount, orderId, customerName, customerPhone) =>
  new Promise(async (resolve) => {
    if (!RAZORPAY_KEY || RAZORPAY_KEY.startsWith("YOUR_")) {
      alert("⚠️ Razorpay key configure nahi hai.");
      return resolve(null);
    }
    const loaded = await loadRazorpayScript();
    if (!loaded || !window.Razorpay) { alert("⚠️ Payment gateway load nahi hua."); return resolve(null); }
    const rzp = new window.Razorpay({
      key: RAZORPAY_KEY, amount: Math.round(amount * 100), currency: "INR",
      name: RESTAURANT.name, description: `Order #${orderId.slice(1, 5).toUpperCase()}`,
      prefill: { name: customerName, contact: customerPhone },
      theme: { color: COLORS.copper },
      handler: (response) => resolve(response),
      modal: { ondismiss: () => resolve(null) }
    });
    rzp.on('payment.failed', () => resolve(null));
    rzp.open();
  });

const processRazorpayPayment = async (amount, orderId, customerName, customerPhone) => {
  try {
    return await HX.payWithRazorpay({
      key: RAZORPAY_KEY, amount, orderId, name: RESTAURANT.name,
      desc: `Order #${orderId.slice(1, 5).toUpperCase()}`,
      prefill: { name: customerName, contact: customerPhone }, color: COLORS.copper
    });
  } catch (e) { alert("⚠️ " + (e.message || e)); return null; }
};
const loadRazorpayScriptOld = () => new Promise((resolve) => {
  const script = document.createElement('script');
  script.src = 'https://checkout.razorpay.com/v1/checkout.js';
  script.onload = () => resolve(true);
  script.onerror = () => resolve(false);
  document.body.appendChild(script);
});

const processRazorpayPaymentOld = async (amount, orderId, customerName, customerPhone) => {
  const loaded = await loadRazorpayScript();
  if (!loaded) { alert("⚠️ Payment gateway could not load."); return false; }
  const options = {
    key: RAZORPAY_KEY, amount: Math.round(amount * 100), currency: "INR",
    name: RESTAURANT.name, description: `Order #${orderId}`,
    prefill: { name: customerName, contact: customerPhone },
    theme: { color: COLORS.copper },
    handler: function (response) { console.log("Payment successful:", response); return true; }
  };
  const razorpay = new window.Razorpay(options);
  razorpay.open();
  return true;
};

const KitchenNotificationColumn = memo(({ orders, selectedOrderId, onSelect }) => { useLiveNow(30000);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const newOrders = orders.filter(o => o.status === "new");
  const preparingOrders = orders.filter(o => o.status === "preparing");

  if (!open) {
    return (
      <button onClick={() => setOpen(true)} aria-label="Open notifications"
        style={{ position: "fixed", top: 90, right: 16, zIndex: 90, background: newOrders.length ? COLORS.copper : COLORS.ink, color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: "50%", width: 52, height: 52, fontSize: 22, cursor: "pointer", boxShadow: "0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)" }}
        className={newOrders.length ? "notification-pulse" : ""}>
        🔔{newOrders.length > 0 && (
          <span style={{ position: "absolute", top: -4, right: -4, background: COLORS.error, color: "#fff", borderRadius: "50%", width: 22, height: 22, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 800, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>{newOrders.length}</span>
        )}
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ position: "fixed", top: 90, right: 16, width: 240, maxHeight: "calc(100vh - 120px)", background: "#fff", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 16, boxShadow: "0 12px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", zIndex: 90, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", overflow: "hidden" }} className="slide-right">
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", padding: "12px 14px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, background: COLORS.paper }}>
        <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 13, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>🔔 Live Orders</span>
        <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", fontSize: 18, cursor: "pointer", color: COLORS.textLight }}>✕</button>
      </div>
      <div style={{ overflowY: "auto", padding: 10, flex: 1 }}>
        {newOrders.length === 0 && preparingOrders.length === 0 && (
          <div style={{ textAlign: "center", padding: "24px 0", color: COLORS.textLight, fontSize: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 28, marginBottom: 6 }}>😌</div>All quiet
          </div>
        )}
        {newOrders.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 800, color: COLORS.copper, letterSpacing: 1, marginBottom: 6, textTransform: "uppercase" }}>🆕 New ({newOrders.length})</div>
            {newOrders.map(o => (
              <button key={o.id} onClick={() => onSelect(o.id)} style={{ width: "100%", textAlign: "left", background: selectedOrderId === o.id ? COLORS.copperLight : COLORS.paper, border: `1px solid ${selectedOrderId === o.id ? COLORS.copper : COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 10px", marginBottom: 6, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: "inherit" }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
                  <strong style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>{o.orderType === "parcel" ? "🛍️" : `T${o.table}`}</strong>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: COLORS.textLight }}>{timeAgo(o.createdAt)}</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.textLight, marginTop: 2 }}>
                  {o.items.length} item{o.items.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""}
                  {o.kots?.length > 1 && (<span style={{ marginLeft: 6, color: COLORS.info, fontWeight: 700 }}>+{o.kots.length - 1} KOT</span>)}
                </div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </>
        )}
        {preparingOrders.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 800, color: COLORS.sage, letterSpacing: 1, margin: "10px 0 6px", textTransform: "uppercase" }}>👨‍🍳 Cooking ({preparingOrders.length})</div>
            {preparingOrders.map(o => (
              <button key={o.id} onClick={() => onSelect(o.id)} style={{ width: "100%", textAlign: "left", background: selectedOrderId === o.id ? COLORS.sageLight : COLORS.paper, border: `1px solid ${selectedOrderId === o.id ? COLORS.sage : COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 10px", marginBottom: 6, cursor: "pointer", fontFamily: "inherit" }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
                  <strong style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>{o.orderType === "parcel" ? "🛍️" : `T${o.table}`}</strong>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: COLORS.textLight }}>{timeAgo(o.createdAt)}</span>
                </div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});

// ==
// 13.5 AI ORDER MODAL (VOICE & TEXT)
// ==

function AIOrderModal({ menu, onOrderParsed, onClose }) {
  const [aiText, setAiText] = useState("");
  const [aiBusy, setAiBusy] = useState(false);
  const [aiErr, setAiErr] = useState("");
  const [listening, setListening] = useState(false);
  const recRef = useRef(null);

  const startMic = () => {
    const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
    if (!SR) {
      setAiErr("⚠️ Is browser me mic support nahi hai. Keyboard ka mic use karein.");
      return;
    }

    // Agar pehle se sun raha hai toh stop karein
    if (listening && recRef.current) {
      try {
        recRef.current.stop();
      } catch (err) {}
      setListening(false);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const r = new SR();
      r.lang = "hi-IN";
      r.interimResults = false;
      r.continuous = false;

      r.onstart = () => {
        setListening(true);
        setAiErr("");
      };

      r.onresult = (e) => {
        const transcript = Array.from(e.results)
          .map((res) => res[0].transcript)
          .join(" ");
        setAiText((prev) => (prev ? prev + " " : "") + transcript);
      };

      r.onerror = (e) => {
        setListening(false);
        if (e.error === "not-allowed") {
          setAiErr("⚠️ Mic permission blocked hai. Settings me allow karein.");
        } else if (e.error === "no-speech") {
          setAiErr("⚠️ Koi aawaz nahi suni gayi. Dobara bolein.");
        } else {
          setAiErr(`⚠️ Mic error: ${e.error || "Aawaz capture nahi hui"}`);
        }
      };

      r.onend = () => {
        setListening(false);
      };

      recRef.current = r;
      r.start();
    } catch (err) {
      setListening(false);
      setAiErr("⚠️ Mic start nahi ho paya. Dobara tap karein.");
    }
  };

  const runAi = async () => {
    const text = aiText.trim();
    if (!text || aiBusy) return;
    setAiBusy(true);
    setAiErr("");
    try {
      const r = await HX.fetchWithTimeout("/api/parse-order", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(auth.currentUser ? { Authorization: "Bearer " + (await auth.currentUser.getIdToken()) } : {}) },

        body: JSON.stringify({
          text,
          menu: (menu || [])
            .filter((m) => m.available)
            .map((m) => ({ id: m.id, name: m.name, portion: m.portion || "" })),
        }),
      });
      const data = await r.json();
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "AI processing fail ho gayi");
      onOrderParsed(HX.sanitizeAiLines(data, menu));
      onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      setAiErr("⚠️ " + (e.message || e));
    } finally {
      setAiBusy(false);
    }
  };


  return (
    <div style={{ position: "fixed", inset: 0, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.65)", zIndex: 280, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", padding: 14 }} onClick={onClose}>
      <div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} style={{ background: "#fff", borderRadius: 18, padding: 18, width: "100%", maxWidth: 390, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: 10 }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center" }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 18, fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif" }}>🤖 AI Order</div>
          <button onClick={onClose} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", fontSize: 18, cursor: "pointer" }}>✕</button>
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#8A8375" }}>Bolo ya likho: "2 veg roll, 1 cold coffee"</div>
        <textarea
          rows={3}
          value={aiText}
          onChange={(e) => setAiText(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Order yahan likhein ya mic se bolein..."
          style={{ width: "100%", padding: 10, borderRadius: 10, border: "1.5px solid #E8E6DC", boxSizing: "border-box", fontSize: 14, resize: "none" }}
        />
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <button type="button" onClick={startMic} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 10, borderRadius: 10, background: listening ? "#C0392B" : "#FAFAF8", border: "1.5px solid #E8E6DC", fontWeight: 800, color: listening ? "#fff" : "#1A1A1A", cursor: "pointer" }}>
            {listening ? "⏹️ Ruko" : "🎤 Bolo"}
          </button>
          <button type="button" onClick={runAi} disabled={aiBusy || !aiText.trim()} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 10, borderRadius: 10, background: "#3B82F6", color: "#fff", border: "none", fontWeight: 800, cursor: "pointer", opacity: (aiBusy || !aiText.trim()) ? 0.6 : 1 }}>
            {aiBusy ? "⏳ Soch raha..." : "✨ Samjho"}
          </button>
        </div>
        {aiErr && <div style={{ color: "#EF4444", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>{aiErr}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
