// 🛍️ Parcel timing: roz 11:00 AM - 10:00 PM (India time)
const PARCEL_OPEN = 11, PARCEL_CLOSE = 22;
const parcelOpenNow = () => {
  const ist = new Date(Date.now() + (new Date().getTimezoneOffset() + 330) * 60000);
  const h = ist.getHours() + ist.getMinutes() / 60;
  return h >= PARCEL_OPEN && h < PARCEL_CLOSE;
};
const PARCEL_MSG = "🛍️ Parcel order roz subah 11 baje se raat 10 baje tak hi liya jata hai. Abhi Dine-in order kar sakte ho.";

const openUpiApp = (amount, ref) => {
  const am = Math.max(0, Math.round((Number(amount) || 0) * 100) / 100);
  const note = "Order " + String(ref || "").replace(/^O/i, "").slice(0, 6).toUpperCase();
  const url = "upi://pay?pa=" + encodeURIComponent(RESTAURANT.upiId) + "&pn=" + encodeURIComponent(RESTAURANT.name) + "&am=" + am + "&cu=INR&tn=" + encodeURIComponent(note);
  const isMobile = /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile/i.test(navigator.userAgent || "");
  if (!isMobile) {
    const qr = "https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=260x260&data=" + encodeURIComponent(url);
    const w = window.open("", "_blank");
    if (w) { w.document.write('<h3>Scan karke pay karo</h3><img src="' + qr + '"/>'); w.document.close(); }
    else alert("UPI ID: " + RESTAURANT.upiId + " Amount: Rs " + am);
    return;
  }
  window.location.href = url;
};

function CustomerView({ menu, orders, placeOrder, bookEvent, gallery, offersList, table, setTable, requestPinPrompt, settings, isDark, setIsDark, requestWaiter, loyaltyRules, loyaltyUsers, coinHistory, setOrdersState, categories, flashSaleItems, comboOffers, setMenuState, setLoyaltyRules, advanceStatus }) {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(categories[0] || "Thali");
  /* ENP-CAT-FIRST */ const [catPickerOpen, setCatPickerOpen] = useState(true);
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useViewMode();
  const cartRef = useRef({});
  const lastStatusRef = useRef({});
  const payIdRef = useRef(null);
  const pendingOrderIdRef = useRef(null);
  const aiTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const waiterLock = usePinLockout();  const [cart, setCart] = useState({});
  const [showAiModal, setShowAiModal] = useState(false);
  const [cartOpen, setCartOpen] = useState(false);
  const [showSidebar, setShowSidebar] = useState(false);
  const [activeModal, setActiveModal] = useState(null);
  const [myOrderIds, setMyOrderIds] = useState([]);
  const [orderType, setOrderType] = useState("dine_in");
  const [custName, setCustName] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem("enp_cust_name") || "");
  const [custPhone, setCustPhone] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem("enp_cust_phone") || "");
  const [custAddress, setCustAddress] = useState("");
  const [custPinCode, setCustPinCode] = useState("");
  const [custExactAddress, setCustExactAddress] = useState("");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [vegOnly, setVegOnly] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [toastType, setToastType] = useState('info');
  const [aiSuggestion, setAiSuggestion] = useState(null);
  const [claimedReward, setClaimedReward] = useState(null);
  const [otpStep, setOtpStep] = useState("phone");
  const [otpCode, setOtpCode] = useState("");
  const [generatedOtp, setGeneratedOtp] = useState("");
  const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem("enp_cust_logged_in") === "true");
  const [couponCode, setCouponCode] = useState("");
  const [appliedDiscount, setAppliedDiscount] = useState(0);
  const [couponMin, setCouponMin] = useState(0);
  const [bookType, setBookType] = useState("table");
  const [bookData, setBookData] = useState({ name: "", phone: "", date: "", time: "", guests: "" });
  const [confirmedBooking, setConfirmedBooking] = useState(null);
  const [favorites, setFavorites] = useLocalStorage('favorites', []);
  const [scheduleDate, setScheduleDate] = useState("");
  const [scheduleTime, setScheduleTime] = useState("");
  const [isScheduled, setIsScheduled] = useState(false);
  const [paymentMethod, setPaymentMethod] = useState("cash");
  const [isProcessingPayment, setIsProcessingPayment] = useState(false);
  const [activeOrderIdForChat, setActiveOrderIdForChat] = useState(null);
  const [runningOrderId, setRunningOrderId] = useState(null);
  const [otpSending, setOtpSending] = useState(false);
  const [resendIn, setResendIn] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (resendIn <= 0) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setResendIn((s) => s - 1), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [resendIn]);

  cartRef.current = cart;
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;
    const visibleCategories = useMemo(() => {
    const hidden = settings?.hiddenCategories || [];
    const actualMenuCats = Array.from(new Set(menu.map((m) => m.category).filter(Boolean)));
    const matched = categories.filter((c) => actualMenuCats.includes(c) && !hidden.includes(c));
    const extras = actualMenuCats.filter((c) => !categories.includes(c) && !hidden.includes(c));
    const finalCats = [...matched, ...extras];
    return finalCats.length > 0 ? finalCats : actualMenuCats;
  }, [categories, menu, settings?.hiddenCategories]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (visibleCategories.length > 0 && !visibleCategories.includes(category)) setCategory(visibleCategories[0]);
  }, [visibleCategories, category]);
  useEffect(() => () => clearTimeout(aiTimerRef.current), []);
 
  const [showWaiterMode, setShowWaiterMode] = useState(wasWaiterOpen);
    const [showWaiterPinModal, setShowWaiterPinModal] = useState(false);
  const [waiterPinInput, setWaiterPinInput] = useState("");
  const [waiterEmail, setWaiterEmail] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
  try { sessionStorage.setItem(WAITER_FLAG, showWaiterMode ? "1" : "0"); } catch (e) {}
}, [showWaiterMode]);
  const [waiterUnlocked, setWaiterUnlocked] = useState(wasWaiterOpen);

  // 🎂 DOB popup (login ke baad)
  const [showDobModal, setShowDobModal] = useState(false);
  const [dobInput, setDobInput] = useState("");
  const [dobSaving, setDobSaving] = useState(false);
  const [dobErr, setDobErr] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLoggedIn || waiterUnlocked) return;
    const p = String(custPhone || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
    if (p.length !== 10) return;
    let off = false;
    (async () => {
      try {
        if (localStorage.getItem("enp_dob_done_" + p) === "1") return;
        const skipAt = Number(localStorage.getItem("enp_dob_skip_" + p) || 0);
        if (Date.now() - skipAt < 3 * 86400000) return;
        let hasDob = false;
        try {
          const s = await getDoc(doc(db, "customers", p));
          hasDob = s.exists() && !!s.data().dob;
        } catch (e) {}
        if (off) return;
        if (hasDob) { localStorage.setItem("enp_dob_done_" + p, "1"); return; }
        setShowDobModal(true);
      } catch (e) {}
    })();
    return () => { off = true; };
  }, [isLoggedIn, custPhone]);
  const saveDob = async () => {
    const p = String(custPhone || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
    if (!dobInput) { setDobErr("Apni date of birth chuno"); return; }
    if (new Date(dobInput) > new Date()) { setDobErr("Future ki date nahi chalegi"); return; }
    setDobErr(""); setDobSaving(true);
    try {
      await setDoc(doc(db, "customers", p), { phone: p, name: custName || "Guest", dob: dobInput, dobSavedAt: Date.now() }, { merge: true });
      try { localStorage.setItem("enp_dob_done_" + p, "1"); } catch (e) {}
      setShowDobModal(false);
      showToast("🎂 Thanks! Birthday pe special offer milega", "success");
    } catch (e) { console.error("DOB save:", e); setDobErr("Save nahi hua: " + (e.code || e.message)); }
    finally { setDobSaving(false); }
  };
  const skipDob = () => {
    const p = String(custPhone || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
    try { localStorage.setItem("enp_dob_skip_" + p, String(Date.now())); } catch (e) {}
    setShowDobModal(false);
  };

useEffect(() => {
  if (!showWaiterMode) return;
  window.history.pushState({ w: 1 }, "");
  const onPop = () => window.history.pushState({ w: 1 }, "");
  window.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
  return () => window.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
}, [showWaiterMode]);

  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    const _hid = settings?.hiddenCategories || [];
    const _veg = !!settings?.vegMode;
    const _vis = (m) => (!_veg || m.veg) && [m.category, ...(m.alsoIn || [])].some((c) => !_hid.includes(c));
    let items = (searchQuery.trim() ? menu : menu.filter((m) => m.category === category || (m.alsoIn || []).includes(category))).filter(_vis);
    items = items.filter((m) => {
      if (vegOnly && !m.veg) return false;
if (searchQuery.trim()) { const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase(); return (m.name || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) || (m.desc || "").toLowerCase().includes(q); }      return true;
    });
    return items;
  }, [menu, category, searchQuery, vegOnly, settings]);

  let emptyReason = 'category_empty';
  if (vegOnly) emptyReason = 'veg_filtered';
  else if (searchQuery && searchQuery.trim() !== '') emptyReason = 'search_no_results';

  const cartItems = Object.entries(cart).filter(([id, entry]) => getCartQty(entry) > 0 && menu.some(m => m.id === id));
const cartCount = cartItems.reduce((s, [, entry]) => s + getCartQty(entry), 0);
const subtotal = cartItems.reduce((s, [id, entry]) => {
  const item = menu.find((m) => m.id === id);
  return s + getCartLineTotal(entry, item);
}, 0);
  const deliveryFee = orderType === "parcel" ? 40 : 0;
  useEffect(() => { if (appliedDiscount > 0 && subtotal < couponMin) setAppliedDiscount(0); }, [appliedDiscount, couponMin, subtotal]);
  const discountAmount = Math.round((subtotal * appliedDiscount) / 100);


  const cartTotal = Math.max(0, subtotal - discountAmount) + deliveryFee;
  const [myLoyalty, setMyLoyalty] = useState(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const _p = HX.cleanPhone(custPhone);
    if (!HX.isValidPhone(_p)) { setMyLoyalty(null); return; }
    let off = false;
    getDoc(doc(db, "loyaltyUsers", _p)).then((s) => { if (!off) setMyLoyalty(s.exists() ? s.data() : null); }).catch(() => {});
    return () => { off = true; };
  }, [custPhone]);
  const activeUser = isLoggedIn ? (loyaltyUsers.find(u => u.phone === custPhone) || myLoyalty) : null;

// 👇 VIP wala code yahan aayega 👇
const isVip = activeUser?.isVip === true;
const vipDiscount = isVip ? Math.round(subtotal * 0.20) : 0; 

const currentCoins = activeUser ? activeUser.coins : 0;
const loyaltyTier = getLoyaltyTier(currentCoins);
const loyaltyDiscount = activeUser && appliedDiscount === 0 ? Math.round(loyaltyTier.discount * subtotal) : 0;

// Final Total (isme vipDiscount minus ho raha hai)
const finalTotal = Math.max(0, subtotal - discountAmount - loyaltyDiscount - vipDiscount) + deliveryFee;
const newEarnedCoins = Math.floor(finalTotal / (Number(loyaltyRules?.rate) || 10));
  const myActiveOrders = orders.filter(o => myOrderIds.includes(o.id) && o.status !== "served" && o.status !== "cancelled");
  const [pastOrders, setPastOrders] = useState([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeModal !== "orderHistory") return;
    const missing = myOrderIds.slice(-15).filter(id => !orders.some(o => o.id === id));
    if (missing.length === 0) return;
    let off = false;
    Promise.all(missing.map(id => getDoc(doc(db, "orders", id)).then(s => (s.exists() ? { id: s.id, ...s.data(), items: s.data().items || [] } : null)).catch(() => null)))
      .then(list => { if (!off) setPastOrders(list.filter(Boolean)); });
    return () => { off = true; };
  }, [activeModal, myOrderIds.join(",")]);

const showToast = useCallback((msg, type = 'info') => {
  const config = TOAST_CONFIG[type] || TOAST_CONFIG.info;
  if (type === 'reward' && navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 50, 100]);
  if (type === 'order') playNotificationSound();

  toastTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), config.duration);

  const handleSetQty = useCallback((id, q) => {
    const prevCart = cartRef.current;
    const oldQty = getCartQty(prevCart[id]);
    const flashRow = flashSaleItems.find(f => f.active && f.id === id && f.stock != null && prevCart[id]?.priceOverride === f.discountPrice);
    if (flashRow && q > flashRow.stock) { showToast(`⚠️ Sirf ${flashRow.stock} bache hain`, 'warning'); return; }
    if (q > oldQty && q === 1) {
      const _s = settingsRef.current || {};
      const options = getSmartSuggestionPool(menu.filter((m) => (!_s.vegMode || m.veg) && !(_s.hiddenCategories || []).includes(m.category)), prevCart);
      if (options.length > 0) {
        setAiSuggestion(options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)]);
        clearTimeout(aiTimerRef.current);
        aiTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setAiSuggestion(null), 6000);
      }
    }

    setCart((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      if (q <= 0) delete next[id];
      else {
        const existingOverride = prev[id]?.priceOverride;
        next[id] = existingOverride != null ? { qty: q, priceOverride: existingOverride, ovQty: prev[id].ovQty } : { qty: q };
      }
      return next;
    });

  const cancelOrder = useCallback(async (orderId) => {
    // 🆕 Check karo ki order abhi bhi "new" status me hai ya nahi

      showToast("⚠️ Khana ban chuka hai (Cooking shuru), ab cancel nahi ho sakta. Restaurant se baat karein.", 'error');
      return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm("Kya aap sach me ye order cancel karna chahte hain?")) return;
    
    try {
      await updateDoc(doc(db, "orders", orderId), { status: "cancelled", cancelledAt: Date.now() });
      setOrdersState(prev => prev.map(o => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, status: "cancelled" } : o));
      showToast("❌ Order cancelled", 'warning');
    } catch (e) {

  const addRunningItems = useCallback(async (orderId, newItems) => {
    try {
      let taggedItems = [];
      let tableNo = "";
      await runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
        const ref = doc(db, "orders", orderId);
        const snap = await tx.get(ref);
        if (!snap.exists()) throw new Error("Order not found");
        const o = snap.data();
        if (o.status === "cancelled") throw new Error("Order cancelled hai");
        const baseKots = (o.kots?.length
          ? o.kots
          : [{ id: uid("kot"), kotNumber: 1, items: o.items, createdAt: o.createdAt, status: o.status }]
        ).map(k => (k.status === "new" && o.status !== "new") ? { ...k, status: o.status } : k);
        const nextNo = Math.max(...baseKots.map(k => k.kotNumber || 1)) + 1;
        taggedItems = newItems.map(it => ({ ...it, kotNumber: nextNo }));
        tableNo = o.table;
        tx.update(ref, {
          kots: [...baseKots, { id: uid("kot"), kotNumber: nextNo, items: taggedItems, createdAt: Date.now(), status: "new", isRunning: true }],
          items: [...(o.items || []), ...taggedItems],
          ...runningPatch(o, taggedItems),
          status: "new",
          lastKotAt: Date.now()
        });
      });
      const addedTotal = taggedItems.reduce((s, it) => s + it.price * it.qty, 0);
      const waText = `🍳 *RUNNING KOT* — Order #${orderId.slice(1, 5).toUpperCase()}\nTable ${tableNo}\n` + taggedItems.map(i => `• ${i.qty}x ${i.name}`).join("\n") + `\n\nAdditional: ₹${addedTotal}`;
      /* WhatsApp band */
      setRunningOrderId(null);
      showToast("🍳 New KOT sent to kitchen!", "success");
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); showToast("⚠️ Failed to add items", "error"); }
  }, [showToast]);

    const order = {
      id: orderId, table: t, orderType: ot || "dine_in",
      customer: { name: customerName, phone: customerPhone || "WALK-IN", address: "" },
      items, waiter: waiterName || "Staff", takenBy: "waiter",

      notes: orderNotes, payment: "cash", paymentStatus: "pending",
      status: "new", paid: false, createdAt: Date.now(),
      coinsClaimed: true, earnedCoins: 0, rewardUsedCoins: 0,
      deliveryFee: 0, loyaltyDiscount: 0, discount: 0,
      subtotal: total, finalTotal: total
    };
    await placeOrder(order);
    playNotificationSound();
    const typeLabel = ot === "parcel" ? "🛍️ *PARCEL ORDER*" : `🍽️ Table ${t}`;

  }, [placeOrder]);

const handleWaiterPinSubmit = async () => {
  if (!waiterEmail.trim() || !waiterPinInput) { showToast("⚠️ Email aur password daalo", "error"); return; }
  try {
    const cred = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, waiterEmail.trim(), waiterPinInput);
    const s = await getDoc(doc(db, "staff", cred.user.uid));
    if (!s.exists()) { await signOut(auth); showToast("❌ Ye account staff/waiter ka nahi hai", "error"); setWaiterPinInput(""); return; }
    setWaiterUnlocked(true); setShowWaiterPinModal(false); setWaiterPinInput(""); setShowWaiterMode(true);
    showToast("🔓 Waiter Mode Unlocked!", "success");
  } catch (e) { console.error(e); showToast("❌ Login fail: " + (e.code || e.message), "error"); setWaiterPinInput(""); }
};
const handleWaiterPinSubmitUnused = () => {
  if (waiterLock.isLocked()) {
    showToast(`⏳ Bahut galat attempts. ${waiterLock.secondsLeft()}s ruko`, "error");
    setWaiterPinInput("");
    return;
  }
  const wPin = (settings?.waiterPin ?? "1234").toString().trim();
  const sPin = (settings?.staffPin ?? "5432").toString().trim();
  const aPin = (settings?.adminPin ?? "9876").toString().trim();
  if (waiterPinInput === wPin || waiterPinInput === sPin || waiterPinInput === aPin) {
    waiterLock.reset();
    setWaiterUnlocked(true);
    setShowWaiterPinModal(false);
    setWaiterPinInput("");
    setShowWaiterMode(true);
    showToast("🔓 Waiter Mode Unlocked!", "success");
  } else {
    waiterLock.registerFail();
    showToast("❌ Incorrect Waiter PIN", "error");
    setWaiterPinInput("");
  }
};

  const otpConfRef = useRef(null);
  const otpRecRef = useRef(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (DEMO_OTP) return;
    const u = auth.currentUser;
    const p = String(custPhone || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
    setIsLoggedIn(!!(u && u.phoneNumber && u.phoneNumber === "+91" + p));
  }, [custPhone]);
  const handleSendOtp = async () => {
    if (otpSending || resendIn > 0) return;
    if (!String(custName || "").trim()) { showToast("⚠️ Pehle apna naam daalo", 'error'); return; }
    const p = String(custPhone || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
    if (p.length !== 10) { showToast("⚠️ 10 digit phone daalo", 'error'); return; }
    if (DEMO_OTP) { handleSendOtpDemo(); return; }
    setOtpSending(true);
    try {
      const u = auth.currentUser || await ensureUser();
      if (u.phoneNumber) { setIsLoggedIn(true); showToast("✅ Phone pehle se verified hai", 'success'); return; }
      if (!u.isAnonymous) { showToast("ℹ️ Staff login chal raha hai - pehle logout karo", 'info'); return; }
      if (!otpRecRef.current) {
        otpRecRef.current = new RecaptchaVerifier(auth, "otp-recaptcha", { size: "invisible" });
      }
      otpConfRef.current = await linkWithPhoneNumber(u, "+91" + p, otpRecRef.current);
      setCustPhone(p); setOtpCode(""); setOtpStep("verify"); setResendIn(30);
      showToast("📩 OTP SMS bheja gaya", 'success');
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("OTP send:", e.code, e.message);
      try { otpRecRef.current && otpRecRef.current.clear(); } catch (x) {}
      otpRecRef.current = null;
      const _b = document.getElementById("otp-recaptcha");
      if (_b) _b.innerHTML = "";
      const m = {
        "auth/operation-not-allowed": "Phone SMS blocked: Firebase me SMS region policy me India allow karo",
        "auth/invalid-phone-number": "Phone number galat hai",
        "auth/too-many-requests": "Bahut try ho gaye, thodi der baad try karo",
        "auth/quota-exceeded": "SMS limit khatam (Firebase quota/billing)",
        "auth/captcha-check-failed": "Captcha fail - domain Authorized domains me add karo",
        "auth/billing-not-enabled": "Real SMS ke liye Firebase billing (Blaze) chahiye"
      };
      showToast("❌ " + (m[e.code] || e.code || e.message), 'error');
    } finally {
      setOtpSending(false);
    }
  };
  const handleSendOtpDemo = () => {
    if (!String(custName || "").trim()) { showToast("⚠️ Pehle apna naam daalo", 'error'); return; }
    if (!custPhone || custPhone.length < 10) { showToast("⚠️ Enter valid phone", 'error'); return; }
    if (!DEMO_OTP) {
      showToast("ℹ️ OTP service abhi connected nahi — naam & phone se order karo", 'info');
      return;
    }
    const code = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000).toString();
    setGeneratedOtp(code);
    setOtpStep("verify");
    showToast(`🔐 Demo OTP: ${code}`, 'success');
  };
  const handleSendOtpOld = () => {
    if (!custPhone || custPhone.length < 10) { showToast("⚠️ Enter valid phone", 'error'); return; }
    const code = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000).toString();
    setGeneratedOtp(code); setOtpStep("verify");
    showToast(`🔐 OTP: ${code}`, 'success');
    alert(`🔐 Demo OTP: ${code}`);
  };
  const handleVerifyOtp = async () => {
    if (!String(custName || "").trim()) { showToast("⚠️ Pehle apna naam daalo", 'error'); return; }
    if (DEMO_OTP && !otpConfRef.current) { handleVerifyOtpDemo(); return; }
    if (!otpConfRef.current) { showToast("⚠️ Pehle OTP bhejo", 'error'); return; }
    const p = String(custPhone || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
    try {
      await otpConfRef.current.confirm(String(otpCode).trim());
    } catch (e) {
      if (e.code === "auth/credential-already-in-use" || e.code === "auth/account-exists-with-different-credential") {
        try {
          const cred = PhoneAuthProvider.credentialFromError(e);
          if (!cred) throw e;
          await signInWithCredential(auth, cred);
        } catch (e2) { showToast("❌ OTP verify fail: " + (e2.code || e2.message), 'error'); return; }
      } else {
        showToast(e.code === "auth/invalid-verification-code" ? "❌ OTP galat hai" : "❌ OTP fail: " + (e.code || e.message), 'error');
        return;
      }
    }
    try { await auth.currentUser.getIdToken(true); } catch (e) {}
    otpConfRef.current = null;
    setIsLoggedIn(true); setOtpStep("phone");
    showToast("✅ Phone verify ho gaya", 'success');
    try {
      const _cref = doc(db, "customers", p);
      const _cs = await getDoc(_cref);
      const _old = _cs.exists() ? _cs.data() : null;
      const _patch = { phone: p, address: custAddress || "", uid: auth.currentUser.uid, lastLogin: Date.now(), loginCount: (Number(_old && _old.loginCount) || 0) + 1 };
      if (!_old) _patch.createdAt = Date.now();
      const _nm = String(custName || "").trim();
      if (_nm) _patch.name = _nm;
      await setDoc(doc(db, "customers", p), _patch, { merge: true });
    } catch (e) {}
  };
  const handleVerifyOtpDemo = () => {
  if (!String(custName || "").trim()) { showToast("⚠️ Pehle apna naam daalo", "error"); return; }
  if (generatedOtp && otpCode === generatedOtp) {
    setIsLoggedIn(true); setOtpStep("phone");
    (async () => {
      try {
        const cleanName = String(custName).trim();
        const userRef = doc(db, "customers", custPhone);
        const userSnap = await getDoc(userRef);
        if (!userSnap.exists()) {
          await setDoc(userRef, { phone: custPhone, name: cleanName, address: custAddress || "", createdAt: Date.now(), lastLogin: Date.now(), totalOrders: 0, totalSpent: 0 });
          showToast("✅ New customer registered!", "success");
        } else {
          await updateDoc(userRef, { lastLogin: Date.now(), name: cleanName });
          showToast("✅ Welcome back!", "success");
        }
        localStorage.setItem("eatpark_customer", JSON.stringify({ name: cleanName, phone: custPhone, address: custAddress, isLoggedIn: true }));
      } catch (error) { console.error(error); }
    })();
  } else showToast("❌ Incorrect OTP", "error");
};
  const handleVerifyOtpOld = () => {
    if (otpCode === generatedOtp || otpCode === "1234") {

      (async () => {

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    if (!window.confirm("Logout karna hai?")) return;
    try { await signOut(auth); } catch (e) { console.error("logout:", e); }
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem("eatpark_customer");

  const handleGetLocation = () => {
    if (!navigator.geolocation) { showToast("Geolocation not supported", 'error'); return; }
    showToast("📍 Fetching location...", 'info');
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      (position) => { 
        const { latitude, longitude } = position.coords; 
        setCustAddress(`Lat: ${latitude.toFixed(5)}, Lng: ${longitude.toFixed(5)}`); 
        showToast("✅ GPS Location aagayi! Ab exact address likhein.", 'success');

  const handleApplyCoupon = () => {
    const code = couponCode.toUpperCase().trim();
    const coupons = {
      "EAT20": { discount: 20, min: 0, ok: () => true, msg: "🎉 20% Applied!" },
      "EATS10": { discount: 10, min: 0, ok: () => true, msg: "🎉 10% Applied!" },
      "WELCOME20": { discount: 20, min: 0, ok: () => myOrderIds.length === 0, msg: "🎉 Welcome 20% Applied!", failMsg: "💳 First order only" },
      "COMEBACK15": { discount: 15, min: 199, ok: () => true, msg: "🎉 15% Applied!" },
      "LOYALTY50": { discount: 50, min: 0, ok: () => currentCoins >= 500, msg: "👑 VIP 50% Applied!", failMsg: "👑 Requires 500+ coins" }
    };
    const coupon = coupons[code];
    if (!coupon) { showToast("❌ Invalid coupon", 'error'); return; }
    if (!coupon.ok()) { showToast(coupon.failMsg || "⚠️ Conditions not met", 'warning'); return; }
    if (subtotal < coupon.min) { showToast(`⚠️ Min ₹${coupon.min} required`, 'warning'); return; }
    setAppliedDiscount(coupon.discount); setCouponMin(coupon.min || 0);
    showToast(coupon.msg, 'success');
  };

  const consumeFlashStock = useCallback(async (lines) => {
    const used = lines.filter(l => flashSaleItems.some(f => f.active && f.id === l.itemId && f.discountPrice === l.price));
    if (used.length === 0) return;
    try {
      await runTransaction(db, async (tx) => {
        const ref = doc(db, "settings", "promotions");
        const snap = await tx.get(ref);
        if (!snap.exists()) return;
        const flash = (snap.data().flashSale || []).map(f => {
          const u = used.find(l => l.itemId === f.id);
          if (!u) return f;
          const left = Math.max(0, (f.stock || 0) - u.qty);
          return { ...f, stock: left, active: left > 0 ? f.active : false };
        });
        tx.update(ref, { flashSale: flash });
      });
    } catch (e) { console.error("Flash stock update failed:", e); }
  }, [flashSaleItems]);

  const handlePlaceOrder = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isProcessingPayment) return;
    if (isScheduled && (!scheduleDate || !scheduleTime)) { showToast("⚠️ Schedule ki date aur time dono daalo", 'error'); return; }
    if (orderType === "parcel" && !parcelOpenNow()) { showToast(PARCEL_MSG, 'error'); return; }
    const _gone = cartItems.filter(([id]) => !menu.some((m) => m.id === id && m.available));
    if (_gone.length > 0) { showToast("⚠️ " + _gone.map(([id]) => (menu.find((m) => m.id === id) || {}).name || "Item").join(", ") + " ab available nahi hai — cart se hata do", 'error'); return; }
    if (cartItems.length === 0) return;
    if (!custName.trim()) { showToast("⚠️ Enter Name", 'error'); return; }
    if (!custPhone.trim() || custPhone.length < 10) { showToast("⚠️ Enter valid Phone", 'error'); return; }
    if (orderType === "parcel" && (!custAddress.trim() || !custExactAddress.trim() || !custPinCode || custPinCode.length !== 6)) { showToast("⚠️ Location, Exact Address aur 6-digit Pincode zaroori hai!", 'error'); return; }

    setIsProcessingPayment(true);
    try {
      if (!pendingOrderIdRef.current) pendingOrderIdRef.current = uid("o");
      const orderId = pendingOrderIdRef.current;
      payIdRef.current = null;
      let paymentInfo = { paid: false, status: "pending", paymentId: null };

      if (paymentMethod === "upi") {
        showToast("📱 UPI app khul raha hai — payment ke baad order confirm karein", 'info');
      }

      const itemStrings = cartItems.map(([id, entry]) => {
        const m = menu.find((x) => x.id === id);
        return `${getCartQty(entry)}x ${m.name}`;
      }).join(", ");

      const initialItems = cartItems.map(([id, entry]) => {
        const m = menu.find((x) => x.id === id);
        return {
          itemId: id, name: m.name, portion: m.portion || "",
          price: Math.round((getCartLineTotal(entry, m) / Math.max(1, getCartQty(entry))) * 100) / 100, originalPrice: m.price,
          qty: getCartQty(entry), kotNumber: 1
        };
      });

      const order = {
        id: orderId, table, orderType,
        customer: { name: custName, phone: custPhone, address: orderType === "parcel" ? custAddress : "", exactAddress: custExactAddress, pincode: custPinCode },
        items: initialItems,
        claimedReward: claimedReward ? claimedReward.item : null,
        rewardUsedCoins: claimedReward ? claimedReward.cost : 0,
        earnedCoins: newEarnedCoins,
        discount: appliedDiscount,
        couponDiscountAmount: discountAmount,
        loyaltyDiscount, loyaltyTier: loyaltyTier.name,
        deliveryFee, subtotal, finalTotal,
        notes, payment: paymentMethod,
        paymentStatus: paymentInfo.status, paymentId: paymentInfo.paymentId,
        status: "new", paid: false,
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        scheduledDate: isScheduled ? scheduleDate : null,
        scheduledTime: isScheduled ? scheduleTime : null,
        isScheduled, coinsClaimed: false,
        kots: [{ id: uid("kot"), kotNumber: 1, items: initialItems, createdAt: Date.now(), status: "new", isRunning: false }]
      };

      await placeOrder(order);

      pendingOrderIdRef.current = null;
      const orderHistory = safeJSON('eatpark_orders', []);
      orderHistory.push(order);
      safeSet('eatpark_orders', orderHistory.slice(-30));

      const customerData = safeJSON('eatpark_customer', {});
      customerData.lastOrderDate = Date.now();
      if (paymentInfo.paid) {
        customerData.totalOrders = (customerData.totalOrders || 0) + 1;
        customerData.totalSpent = (customerData.totalSpent || 0) + finalTotal;
      }
      safeSet('eatpark_customer', customerData);
      try {
        const _cPatch = { lastOrderDate: Date.now() };
        if (paymentInfo.paid) {
          _cPatch.totalOrders = customerData.totalOrders;
          _cPatch.totalSpent = customerData.totalSpent;
        }
        await updateDoc(doc(db, "customers", custPhone), _cPatch);
      } catch (e) { }

      if (paymentMethod === "upi") setTimeout(() => openUpiApp(finalTotal, orderId), 700);
      consumeFlashStock(initialItems);

      const claimedText = claimedReward ? `\n🎁 *Free Reward:* ${claimedReward.item}` : "";
      const scheduleText = isScheduled && scheduleDate && scheduleTime ? `\n📅 *Scheduled:* ${scheduleDate} at ${scheduleTime}` : "";
      const waText = `🚨 *NEW ORDER* (#${orderId.slice(1, 5).toUpperCase()})\n\n*Type:* ${orderType === 'parcel' ? '🛍️ Parcel' : `🍽️ Table ${table}`}\n*Customer:* ${custName} (${custPhone})\n` + (orderType === 'parcel' ? `*Address:* ${custAddress}\n\n` : `\n`) + `*Items:* ${itemStrings}${claimedText}\n` + (appliedDiscount > 0 ? `*Coupon:* ${appliedDiscount}%\n` : ``) + (loyaltyDiscount > 0 ? `*Loyalty:* ${loyaltyTier.name}\n` : ``) + `*Total:* ₹${finalTotal}\n*Payment:* ${paymentMethod} (${paymentInfo.paid ? "PAID" : "PENDING"})\n` + scheduleText + (notes ? `\n*Notes:* ${notes}` : ``);
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = `https://wa.me/${RESTAURANT.whatsapp}?text=${encodeURIComponent(waText)}`;
      /* WhatsApp band */

      playNotificationSound();
      sendPushNotification("🛎️ New Order", `Order #${orderId.slice(1, 5)} - ₹${finalTotal}`);
      setMyOrderIds(prev => [...prev, order.id]);
      setCart({});
      setNotes(""); setClaimedReward(null); setCartOpen(false); setActiveModal('track');
      setIsScheduled(false); setScheduleDate(""); setScheduleTime("");
      showToast("🎉 Order Placed!", 'success');
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Order error:", e);
      if (payIdRef.current) alert("⚠️ Payment ho gaya hai par order save nahi hua.\nPayment ID: " + payIdRef.current + "\nYe ID staff ko dikhao.");
      else showToast("⚠️ " + authMsg(e), 'error');
    } finally { setIsProcessingPayment(false); }
  }, [isProcessingPayment, cartItems, custName, custPhone, custAddress, orderType, table, paymentMethod, finalTotal, subtotal, discountAmount, deliveryFee, claimedReward, newEarnedCoins, isScheduled, scheduleDate, scheduleTime, appliedDiscount, loyaltyDiscount, loyaltyTier, notes, menu, placeOrder, sendPushNotification, consumeFlashStock, showToast]);

  const handlePlaceOrderOld = useCallback(async () => {
    if (cartItems.length === 0) return;

  const m = menu.find((mi) => mi.id === id);
  return `${getCartQty(entry)}x ${m.name}`;

      const claimedText = claimedReward ? `\n🎁 *Free Reward:* ${claimedReward.item}` : "";

      const waText = `🚨 *NEW ORDER* (#${orderId.slice(1, 5).toUpperCase()})\n\n*Type:* ${orderType === 'parcel' ? '🛍️ Parcel' : `🍽️ Table ${table}`}\n*Customer:* ${custName} (${custPhone})\n` + (orderType === 'parcel' ? `*Address:* ${custAddress}\n\n` : `\n`) + `*Items:* ${itemStrings}${claimedText}\n` + (appliedDiscount > 0 ? `*Coupon:* ${appliedDiscount}%\n` : ``) + (loyaltyDiscount > 0 ? `*Loyalty:* ${loyaltyTier.name}\n` : ``) + `*Total:* ₹${finalTotal}\n*Payment:* ${paymentMethod}\n` + scheduleText + (notes ? `*Notes:* ${notes}` : ``);

      link.target = '_blank'; document.body.appendChild(link); link.click(); document.body.removeChild(link);

      const initialItems = cartItems.map(([id, entry]) => {
  const m = menu.find((mi) => mi.id === id);
  const qty = getCartQty(entry);
  const finalPrice = entry.priceOverride ?? m.price;
  return {
    itemId: id,
    name: m.name,
    portion: m.portion || "",
    price: finalPrice,
    originalPrice: m.price,
    qty,
    kotNumber: 1
  };
});

      const order = {
        id: orderId, table, orderType,
        customer: { name: custName, phone: custPhone, address: orderType === "parcel" ? custAddress : "" },

        claimedReward: claimedReward ? claimedReward.item : null,
        rewardUsedCoins: claimedReward ? claimedReward.cost : 0,
        earnedCoins: newEarnedCoins,
        discount: appliedDiscount,
        loyaltyDiscount, loyaltyTier: loyaltyTier.name,
        deliveryFee, notes, payment: paymentMethod,
        paymentStatus: paymentMethod === "cash" ? "pending" : "paid",
        status: "new", paid: paymentMethod !== "cash",
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        scheduledDate: isScheduled ? scheduleDate : null,
        scheduledTime: isScheduled ? scheduleTime : null,
        isScheduled,
        coinsClaimed: false,
        kots: [{ id: uid("kot"), kotNumber: 1, items: initialItems, createdAt: Date.now(), status: "new", isRunning: false }]
      };

      const orderHistory = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('eatpark_orders') || '[]');
      orderHistory.push(order);
      localStorage.setItem('eatpark_orders', JSON.stringify(orderHistory));

      const customerData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('eatpark_customer') || '{}');
      customerData.totalOrders = (customerData.totalOrders || 0) + 1;
      customerData.totalSpent = (customerData.totalSpent || 0) + finalTotal;
      customerData.lastOrderDate = Date.now();
      localStorage.setItem('eatpark_customer', JSON.stringify(customerData));

      await placeOrder(order, claimedReward ? claimedReward.cost : 0);
      try { await updateDoc(doc(db, "customers", custPhone), { totalOrders: customerData.totalOrders, totalSpent: customerData.totalSpent, lastOrderDate: Date.now() }); } catch (e) { }

      playNotificationSound();
      sendPushNotification("🛎️ New Order", `Order #${orderId.slice(1, 5)} - ₹${finalTotal}`);
      setMyOrderIds([...myOrderIds, order.id]);
      setCart({});
      localStorage.removeItem('eatpark_cart');
      setNotes(""); setClaimedReward(null); setCartOpen(false); setActiveModal('track');
      setIsScheduled(false); setScheduleDate(""); setScheduleTime("");

    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Order error:", e);
      showToast("⚠️ Failed. Try again.", 'error');
    } finally { setIsProcessingPayment(false); }
  }, [cartItems, custName, custPhone, custAddress, orderType, table, paymentMethod, finalTotal, claimedReward, isScheduled, scheduleDate, scheduleTime, appliedDiscount, loyaltyDiscount, loyaltyTier, notes, menu, placeOrder, sendPushNotification, myOrderIds, showToast]);

 const handleBooking = async () => {
  if (!bookData.name || !bookData.phone || !bookData.date || !bookData.time || !bookData.guests) { 
    showToast("⚠️ Fill all fields", 'error'); 
    return; 
  }

  const newBooking = { 
    ...bookData, 
    type: bookType, 
    id: uid("b"), 
    status: "pending", 
    createdAt: Date.now() 
  };

  try { await bookEvent(newBooking); } catch (e) { console.error(e); showToast("⚠️ Booking send nahi hui. Internet check karo.", 'error'); return; }

  // 📲 WhatsApp notification to restaurant
  const bookingType = bookType === "party" ? "🎉 PARTY BOOKING" : "🍽️ TABLE BOOKING";
  const waText = 
    `*${bookingType} REQUEST*%0A%0A` +
    `*Name:* ${bookData.name}%0A` +
    `*Phone:* ${bookData.phone}%0A` +
    `*Date:* ${bookData.date}%0A` +
    `*Time:* ${bookData.time}%0A` +
    `*Guests:* ${bookData.guests}%0A` +
    `*Booking ID:* #${newBooking.id.slice(1, 6).toUpperCase()}%0A%0A` +
    `_Sent from Eat & Park App_`;

  window.open(
    `https://wa.me/${RESTAURANT.whatsapp}?text=${encodeURIComponent(waText.replace(/%0A/g, "\n"))}`,
    "_blank"
  );

  setConfirmedBooking(newBooking);
  setBookData({ name: "", phone: "", date: "", time: "", guests: "" });
  showToast("✅ Booking Sent! WhatsApp kholo aur Send dabao.", 'success');
};
  const addComboToCart = useCallback((combo) => {
    try {
      const lines = (combo.items || []).map(it => ({ ...it, quantity: Number(it.quantity) || 1, menuItem: menu.find(m => m.id === it.id) }));
      if (lines.length === 0 || lines.some(l => !l.menuItem || !l.menuItem.available)) { showToast("⚠️ Combo ka koi item available nahi", 'warning'); return; }
      const totalMenuPrice = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.menuItem.price * l.quantity, 0);
      const finalPrice = Number(combo.finalPrice);
      if (!(totalMenuPrice > 0) || !Number.isFinite(finalPrice)) { showToast("⚠️ Combo ka price set nahi hai", 'warning'); return; }
      const ratio = finalPrice / totalMenuPrice;
      setCart(prev => {
        const next = { ...prev };
        lines.forEach(l => {
          const prevEntry = next[l.id];
          const prevQty = getCartQty(prevEntry);
          const prevPrice = prevEntry?.priceOverride ?? l.menuItem.price;
          const newQty = prevQty + l.quantity;
          const _k = prevEntry?.priceOverride != null ? (prevEntry.ovQty ?? prevQty) : 0;
          const _o = prevEntry?.priceOverride != null ? prevEntry.priceOverride : 0;
          const _cp = l.menuItem.price * ratio;
          next[l.id] = { qty: newQty, priceOverride: Math.round((_k * _o + l.quantity * _cp) / (_k + l.quantity)), ovQty: _k + l.quantity };
        });
        return next;
      });
      showToast(`🎉 ${combo.name} added!`, 'success');
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Combo error:", e);
      showToast("⚠️ Combo error: " + (e?.message || e), 'error');
    }
  }, [menu, showToast]);
  const addComboToCartPrev = useCallback((combo) => {
    const lines = combo.items.map(it => ({ ...it, menuItem: menu.find(m => m.id === it.id) }));
    if (lines.some(l => !l.menuItem || !l.menuItem.available)) { showToast("⚠️ Combo ka koi item available nahi", 'warning'); return; }
    const totalMenuPrice = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.menuItem.price * l.quantity, 0);
    if (totalMenuPrice <= 0) return;
    const ratio = combo.finalPrice / totalMenuPrice;
    setCart(prev => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      lines.forEach(l => {
        next[l.id] = { qty: getCartQty(next[l.id]) + l.quantity, priceOverride: Math.round(l.menuItem.price * ratio) };
      });
      return next;
    });
    showToast(`🎉 ${combo.name} added!`, 'success');
  }, [menu, showToast]);
  const addComboToCartOld = useCallback((combo) => {
  const totalMenuPrice = combo.items.reduce((s, it) => s + it.price * it.quantity, 0);
  const ratio = combo.finalPrice / totalMenuPrice;

  setCart(prev => {
    const next = { ...prev };
    combo.items.forEach(item => {
      const existing = next[item.id];
      const newQty = getCartQty(existing) + item.quantity;
      const newOverride = Math.round(item.price * ratio);
      next[item.id] = { qty: newQty, priceOverride: newOverride };
    });
    return next;
  });
  showToast(`🎉 ${combo.name} added!`, 'success');
}, [showToast]);

  const addFlashSaleToCart = useCallback((item) => {
    const menuItem = menu.find(m => m.id === item.id);
    if (!menuItem || !menuItem.available) { showToast("⚠️ Item available nahi", 'warning'); return; }
    if (item.stock != null && getCartQty(cartRef.current[item.id]) + 1 > item.stock) {
      showToast(`⚠️ Sirf ${item.stock} bache hain`, 'warning'); return;
    }
    setCart(prev => {
      const e = prev[item.id];
      const q = getCartQty(e);
      const k = e?.priceOverride != null ? (e.ovQty ?? q) : 0;
      const o = e?.priceOverride != null ? e.priceOverride : 0;
      return { ...prev, [item.id]: { qty: q + 1, priceOverride: Math.round((k * o + item.discountPrice) / (k + 1)), ovQty: k + 1 } };
    });
    showToast(`⚡ ${item.name} added!`, 'success');
  }, [menu, showToast]);
  const addFlashSaleToCartOld = useCallback((item) => {
  setCart(prev => {
    const existing = prev[item.id];
    const qty = getCartQty(existing) + 1;
    return {
      ...prev,
      [item.id]: { qty, priceOverride: item.discountPrice }
    };
  });
  showToast(`⚡ ${item.name} added!`, 'success');
}, [showToast]);

  const inputStyle = { padding: "12px 16px", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, width: "100%", boxSizing: "border-box" };

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: viewMode === "desktop" ? 1100 : 480, margin: "0 auto", paddingBottom: cartCount ? 120 : 60, background: "#0B111A", color: "#fff", minHeight: "100vh", position: "relative" }}>
      {toast && <Toast message={toast} type={toastType} />}

      
      {toast && <Toast message={toast} type={toastType} />}

      {/* ENP-DARK-LOGIN */}

                  {otpSending ? "⏳ Bhej raha hai..." : resendIn > 0 ? "Dobara bhejo (" + resendIn + "s)" : "Send OTP"}
                </button>

                <label style={{ display: "block", fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 800, color: "#8B9BB4", textTransform: "uppercase", marginBottom: 8, letterSpacing: 0.5, textAlign: "center" }}>OTP {custPhone} par bheja gaya</label>

"Resend OTP (" + resendIn + "s)" : "🔁 Resend OTP"}</button>

              <button onClick={() => requestPinPrompt("admin")} style={{ flex: 1, padding: "10px 0", background: "transparent", border: "1px solid #273449", borderRadius: 12, color: "#8B9BB4", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 12, cursor: "pointer" }}>Admin</button>

"wait" : "pointer", opacity: dobSaving ? 0.6 : 1, boxShadow: "0 8px 20px rgba(255,90,31,0.3)" }}>

      )}

      {/* ENP-CUSTOMER-UI */}
      <style>{"body{background:#0B111A} .slide-up { color: #111827 !important; } .slide-up div, .slide-up span { color: inherit; }.enp-hs::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}.enp-hs{scrollbar-width:none}"}</style>

        <button onClick={() => setShowSidebar(true)} aria-label="Menu" style={{ width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid #273449", background: "#161F2F", color: "#fff", fontSize: 18, cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0 }}>☰</button>
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 17, color: "#fff", whiteSpace: "nowrap", overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis" }}>Eat & Park</div>

        <button onClick={() => { if (waiterUnlocked) setShowWaiterMode(true); else setShowWaiterPinModal(true); }} title="Waiter Mode" style={{ width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid #273449", background: waiterUnlocked ? "#3B82F6" : "#161F2F", color: "#fff", fontSize: 17, cursor: "pointer", flexShrink: 0 }}>{waiterUnlocked ? "🧑‍🍳" : "🔒"}</button>

          <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif", fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 800, color: "#fff", marginBottom: 8 }}>{RESTAURANT.name}</div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            <a href={"tel:" + RESTAURANT.phones[0]} style={{ background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.14)", color: "#fff", padding: "5px 12px", borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: "none" }}>📞 Call</a>

          </div>

      {!searchQuery.trim() && flashSaleItems && flashSaleItems.filter((f) => f.active && (f.stock == null || f.stock > 0)).length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ paddingTop: 18 }}>
          <div style={{ padding: "0 14px 10px", fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif", fontSize: 17, fontWeight: 800, color: "#EF4444" }}>⚡ Flash Sale</div>

              </div>

      <div style={{ padding: "18px 14px 12px", display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
        <div style={{ position: "relative", flex: 1 }}>
          <input type="text" value={searchQuery} onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="Dish search karo..." className="keep-color" style={{ width: "100%", boxSizing: "border-box", padding: "13px 40px 13px 42px", background: "#161F2F", border: "1px solid #273449", borderRadius: 14, color: "#fff", fontSize: 15, outline: "none" }} />
          <span style={{ position: "absolute", left: 14, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", fontSize: 16 }}>🔍</span>
          {searchQuery && <button onClick={() => setSearchQuery("")} style={{ position: "absolute", right: 8, top: "50%", transform: "translateY(-50%)", background: "none", border: "none", color: "#8B9BB4", fontSize: 16, cursor: "pointer", padding: 6 }}>✕</button>}
        </div>

"#0A2A20" : "#161F2F", color: vegOnly ? "#10B981" : "#8B9BB4", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 13, display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, cursor: "pointer", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>

      {!searchQuery.trim() && !catPickerOpen && (

          <button onClick={() => setCatPickerOpen(true)} style={{ flexShrink: 0, padding: "8px 14px", borderRadius: 999, border: "1px solid #273449", background: "#161F2F", color: "#FF8C5A", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 13, cursor: "pointer" }}>← Categories</button>

"#FF5A1F" : "#273449"), background: on ? "#FF5A1F" : "#161F2F", color: on ? "#fff" : "#8B9BB4", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13, cursor: "pointer", boxShadow: on ? "0 6px 16px rgba(255,90,31,0.3)" : "none" }}>{c}</button>

                  <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, background: "linear-gradient(to top, rgba(11,17,26,0.95) 10%, rgba(11,17,26,0.2) 100%)" }} />
                  <div style={{ position: "absolute", left: 12, right: 12, bottom: 10, textAlign: "left" }}>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 800, color: "#fff", lineHeight: 1.2 }}>{c}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "#FF8C5A", fontWeight: 700, marginTop: 2 }}>{catItems.length} items →</div>

        {!searchQuery.trim() && filteredItems.length > 0 && (
