                  <div key={it._rowId || idx} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '10px 4px', borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}` }}>
                    <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                        {it.name} {it.portion ? <span style={{ color: COLORS.textLight, fontSize: 11 }}>({it.portion})</span> : null}
                      </div>
                      <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.copper, fontWeight: 700 }}>₹{it.price} × {it.qty} = ₹{it.price * it.qty}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.sage}`, borderRadius: 8, padding: '2px' }}>
                      <button onClick={() => {
                        const updated = [...billEditItems];
                        if (updated[idx].qty <= 1) updated.splice(idx, 1);
                        else updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], qty: updated[idx].qty - 1 };
                        setBillEditItems(updated);
                      }} style={{ width: 26, height: 26, border: 'none', background: 'transparent', color: COLORS.sage, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 800, cursor: 'pointer' }}>−</button>
                      <span style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 13, minWidth: 16, textAlign: 'center' }}>{it.qty}</span>
                      <button onClick={() => {
                        const updated = [...billEditItems];
                        updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], qty: updated[idx].qty + 1 };
                        setBillEditItems(updated);
                      }} style={{ width: 26, height: 26, border: 'none', background: 'transparent', color: COLORS.sage, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 800, cursor: 'pointer' }}>+</button>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => {
                      if (window.confirm(`Remove "${it.name}"?`)) {
                        setBillEditItems(billEditItems.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
                      }
                    }} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.error}`, color: COLORS.error, width: 32, height: 32, borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 14, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 800 }}>✕</button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* GST */}
            <div style={{ background: COLORS.paper, borderRadius: 14, padding: 14, marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: 0.5, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 10 }}>🧾 GST</div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                {[0, 5, 12, 18].map((rate) => (
                  <button key={rate} onClick={() => setBillGstRate(rate)}
                    style={{
                      padding: '8px 14px', borderRadius: 10,
                      border: `2px solid ${billGstRate === rate ? COLORS.copper : COLORS.line}`,
                      background: billGstRate === rate ? COLORS.copperLight : '#fff',
                      color: billGstRate === rate ? COLORS.copperDark : COLORS.text,
                      fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 13, cursor: 'pointer',
                    }}>
                    {rate === 0 ? 'No GST' : `${rate}%`}
                  </button>
                ))}
                <button onClick={() => setBillGstRate('custom')}
                  style={{
                    padding: '8px 14px', borderRadius: 10,
                    border: `2px solid ${billGstRate === 'custom' ? COLORS.copper : COLORS.line}`,
                    background: billGstRate === 'custom' ? COLORS.copperLight : '#fff',
                    color: billGstRate === 'custom' ? COLORS.copperDark : COLORS.text,
                    fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 13, cursor: 'pointer',
                  }}>
                  Custom
                </button>
                {billGstRate === 'custom' && (
                  <input type="number" placeholder="Rate %" value={billGstCustom} onChange={(e) => setBillGstCustom(e.target.value)}
                    style={{ padding: '8px 12px', border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 14, width: 100, fontWeight: 800 }} />
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* DISCOUNT */}
            <div style={{ background: COLORS.paper, borderRadius: 14, padding: 14, marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: 0.5, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 10 }}>🎁 Discount</div>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10 }}>
                <div>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: COLORS.textLight, display: 'block', marginBottom: 4 }}>Percent (%)</label>
                  <input type="number" min={0} max={100} value={billDiscountPct || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setBillDiscountPct(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value) || 0)))}
                    placeholder="0"
                    style={{ padding: 10, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 14, width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontWeight: 700 }} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, color: COLORS.textLight, display: 'block', marginBottom: 4 }}>Flat (₹)</label>
                  <input type="number" min={0} value={billDiscount || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => setBillDiscount(Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value) || 0))}
                    placeholder="0"
                    style={{ padding: 10, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 14, width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontWeight: 700 }} />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* PAYMENT */}
            <div style={{ background: COLORS.paper, borderRadius: 14, padding: 14, marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: 0.5, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 10 }}>💳 Mode of Payment</div>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                {[
                  { id: 'cash', icon: '💵', label: 'Cash' },
                  { id: 'upi', icon: '📱', label: 'UPI' },
                  { id: 'card', icon: '💳', label: 'Card' },
                  { id: 'razorpay', icon: '⚡', label: 'Razorpay' },
                  { id: 'due', icon: '⏳', label: 'Due' },
                ].map((m) => (
                  <button key={m.id} onClick={() => setBillPayMode(m.id)}
                    style={{
                      padding: '10px 6px', borderRadius: 10,
                      border: `2px solid ${billPayMode === m.id ? COLORS.copper : COLORS.line}`,
                      background: billPayMode === m.id ? COLORS.copperLight : '#fff',
                      color: billPayMode === m.id ? COLORS.copperDark : COLORS.text,
                      fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 12, cursor: 'pointer',
                      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4,
                    }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>{m.icon}</span>
                    <span>{m.label}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
                {['paid', 'pending'].map((s) => (
                  <button key={s} onClick={() => setBillPayStatus(s)}
                    style={{
                      flex: 1, padding: '10px', borderRadius: 10,
                      border: `2px solid ${billPayStatus === s ? (s === 'paid' ? COLORS.success : COLORS.warning) : COLORS.line}`,
                      background: billPayStatus === s ? (s === 'paid' ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.15)' : 'rgba(255,152,0,0.15)') : '#fff',
                      color: billPayStatus === s ? (s === 'paid' ? COLORS.success : '#E65100') : COLORS.text,
                      fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 13, cursor: 'pointer',
                    }}>
                    {s === 'paid' ? '✓ Paid' : '⏳ Pending'}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* BILL NO TOGGLE + NOTES */}
            <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, cursor: 'pointer', marginBottom: 12, padding: 10, background: COLORS.paper, borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <input type="checkbox" checked={billShowBillNo} onChange={(e) => setBillShowBillNo(e.target.checked)} style={{ width: 18, height: 18 }} />
              <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13 }}>🧾 Print Bill Number on receipt</span>
            </label>

            <div style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: 0.5, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 6 }}>📝 Notes</div>
              <textarea placeholder="Special instructions / bill notes..." value={billNotes} onChange={(e) => setBillNotes(e.target.value)} rows={2}
                style={{ padding: 12, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 14, width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box', resize: 'none' }} />
            </div>

            {/* TOTALS PREVIEW */}
            <div style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #fdfbfb, #ebedee)', borderRadius: 14, padding: 16, marginBottom: 16, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.gold}` }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 13, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                <span>Subtotal</span>
                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontWeight: 700 }}>₹{billCalc.sub}</span>
              </div>
              {billDiscountPct > 0 && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 13, color: COLORS.success, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  <span>Discount ({billDiscountPct}%)</span>
                  <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontWeight: 700 }}>-₹{billCalc.discPctAmt}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              {billDiscount > 0 && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 13, color: COLORS.success, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  <span>Extra Discount</span>
                  <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontWeight: 700 }}>-₹{billCalc.discAmt}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              {billCalc.gstRate > 0 && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 13, color: COLORS.copper, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  <span>GST @ {billCalc.gstRate}%</span>
                  <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", fontWeight: 700 }}>+₹{billCalc.gst}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 800, borderTop: `2px solid ${COLORS.ink}`, paddingTop: 10, marginTop: 6 }}>
                <span>GRAND TOTAL</span>
                <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: COLORS.copper }}>₹{billCalc.total}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* ACTIONS */}
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 10, marginBottom: 10 }}>
              <button onClick={async () => {
                const saved = await saveBillEdits();
                if (saved) setBillOrder(null);
              }}
                style={{ padding: 14, borderRadius: 12, border: `2px solid ${COLORS.sage}`, background: 'transparent', color: COLORS.sage, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 14, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                💾 Save Only
              </button>
              <button onClick={async () => {
                const saved = await saveBillEdits();
                if (saved) {
                  setBillOrder(null);
                  setTimeout(() => printFinalBill(saved, billEditItems), 200);
                }
              }}
                style={{ padding: 14, borderRadius: 12, border: 'none', background: COLORS.info, color: '#fff', fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 14, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                🖨️ Save & Print
              </button>
            </div>
            <button onClick={async () => {
              const saved = await saveBillEdits();
              if (saved) {
                if (billOrder.status === "ready") await advanceStatus(billOrder.id, "ready");
                setBillOrder(null);
                setTimeout(() => printFinalBill(saved, billEditItems), 200);
              }
            }}
              style={{ width: '100%', padding: 16, borderRadius: 14, border: 'none', background: COLORS.sage, color: '#fff', fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 15, cursor: 'pointer', marginBottom: 10 }}>
              ✓ Mark Paid & Print Bill
            </button>

            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.textLight, textAlign: 'center', lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
              💡 Save karne se bill Firestore mein update hoga. Print aapke printer pe jayega.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// 18. ADMIN VIEW — HELPER COMPONENTS

function KitchenMetrics({ filteredOrders }) {
  const servedOrders = filteredOrders.filter(o => o.status === "served");
  if (servedOrders.length === 0) return null;
  const hourCounts = {};
  filteredOrders.forEach(o => { const hr = new Date(o.createdAt).getHours(); hourCounts[hr] = (hourCounts[hr] || 0) + 1; });
  const peakHourEntry = Object.entries(hourCounts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
  const peakHourLabel = peakHourEntry ? `${peakHourEntry[0]}:00` : "—";
  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr))", gap: 16, marginBottom: 28 }}>
      <StatCard label="Completed" value={servedOrders.length} icon="✅" color={COLORS.sage} />
      <StatCard label="Peak Hour" value={peakHourLabel} icon="🔥" color={COLORS.copper} />
      <StatCard label="Total" value={filteredOrders.length} icon="📋" color={COLORS.gold} />
    </div>
  );
}

function InventoryAlertBanner({ inventory }) {
  const critical = inventory.filter(i => i.stock < 2);
  const warning = inventory.filter(i => i.stock >= 2 && i.stock < 5);
  if (critical.length === 0 && warning.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
      {critical.length > 0 && (<div style={{ background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)', border: `2px solid ${COLORS.error}`, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}><h4 style={{ color: COLORS.error, margin: '0 0 10px' }}>🚨 CRITICAL</h4>{critical.map(item => (<div key={item.id} style={{ color: COLORS.error, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14 }}>❌ {item.name}: {item.stock} {item.unit}</div>))}</div>)}
      {warning.length > 0 && (<div style={{ background: 'rgba(255, 152, 0, 0.1)', border: `2px solid ${COLORS.warning}`, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12 }}><h4 style={{ color: COLORS.warning, margin: '0 0 10px' }}>⚠️ LOW STOCK</h4>{warning.map(item => (<div key={item.id} style={{ color: COLORS.warning, fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 14 }}>⚠️ {item.name}: {item.stock} {item.unit}</div>))}</div>)}
    </div>
  );
}

function PresetListEditor({ title, hint, items, onChange, placeholder, number, suffix }) {
  const [val, setVal] = useState("");
  const add = () => {
    const raw = val.trim();
    if (!raw) return;
    const v = number ? Number(raw) : raw;
    if (number && (!Number.isFinite(v) || v < 0)) { alert("⚠️ Sahi number daalo"); return; }
    if (items.some((x) => String(x).toLowerCase() === String(v).toLowerCase())) { setVal(""); return; }
    onChange([...items, v]);
    setVal("");
  };
  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#fff", border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 14, padding: 16, marginBottom: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 16 }}>{title}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 10 }}>{hint}</div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", marginBottom: 10 }}>
        {items.length === 0 && <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: COLORS.textLight }}>Abhi koi nahi</span>}
        {items.map((it) => (
          <span key={String(it)} style={{ display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 6, background: COLORS.paper2, borderRadius: 20, padding: "5px 6px 5px 12px", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13 }}>
            {it}{suffix || ""}
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { if (window.confirm(`"${it}" delete karna hai?`)) onChange(items.filter((x) => x !== it)); }}
              style={{ background: COLORS.error, color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: "50%", width: 22, height: 22, fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 800, padding: 0 }}>✕</button>
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
        <input type={number ? "number" : "text"} placeholder={placeholder} value={val} onChange={(e) => setVal(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") add(); }}
          style={{ padding: "10px 12px", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 14, flex: 1, boxSizing: "border-box" }} />
        <button type="button" onClick={add} style={{ background: COLORS.copper, color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: 10, padding: "10px 18px", fontWeight: 800, cursor: "pointer" }}>+ Add</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PresetsAdmin() {
  const [presets, savePresets] = usePresets();
  const [cName, setCName] = useState("");
  const [cPhone, setCPhone] = useState("");
  const customers = presets.customers || [];
  const addCustomer = () => {
    if (!cName.trim()) { alert("⚠️ Customer ka naam daalo"); return; }
    savePresets({ ...presets, customers: [...customers, { name: cName.trim(), phone: cPhone.trim() }] });
    setCName(""); setCPhone("");
  };
  const fld = { padding: "10px 12px", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 14, boxSizing: "border-box" };
  return (
    <div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 16, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
        Yahan ek baar daalo, hamesha save rahega. Staff ke bill screen me sirf select karna padega.
      </div>
      <PresetListEditor title="🧑‍🍳 Waiters" hint="Bill screen me chips ki tarah dikhenge" placeholder="Waiter ka naam"
        items={presets.waiters || []} onChange={(l) => savePresets({ ...presets, waiters: l })} />
      <div style={{ background: "#fff", border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 14, padding: 16, marginBottom: 16 }}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 16 }}>👤 Saved Customers</div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 10 }}>Select karte hi naam + phone bhar jayega</div>
        {customers.length === 0 && <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 10 }}>Abhi koi nahi</div>}
        {customers.map((c, i) => (
          <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", background: COLORS.paper2, borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 12px", marginBottom: 6, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 13 }}>
            <span>{c.name}{c.phone ? ` · ${c.phone}` : ""}</span>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => { if (window.confirm(`"${c.name}" delete karna hai?`)) savePresets({ ...presets, customers: customers.filter((_, k) => k !== i) }); }}
              style={{ background: COLORS.error, color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: "50%", width: 24, height: 24, fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 800, padding: 0 }}>✕</button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", marginTop: 8 }}>
          <input placeholder="Naam" value={cName} onChange={(e) => setCName(e.target.value)} style={{ ...fld, flex: 1, minWidth: 120 }} />
          <input placeholder="Phone" value={cPhone} onChange={(e) => setCPhone(e.target.value)} style={{ ...fld, flex: 1, minWidth: 120 }} />
          <button type="button" onClick={addCustomer} style={{ background: COLORS.copper, color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: 10, padding: "10px 18px", fontWeight: 800, cursor: "pointer" }}>+ Add</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <PresetListEditor title="📝 Quick Notes" hint="Jaise: Kam teekha, Bina pyaaz — bill me tap karke notes me judega" placeholder="Note likho"
        items={presets.notes || []} onChange={(l) => savePresets({ ...presets, notes: l })} />
      <PresetListEditor title="🎁 Discount %" hint="Bill me ek tap se discount lagega" placeholder="Jaise 10" number suffix="%"
        items={presets.discounts || []} onChange={(l) => savePresets({ ...presets, discounts: l })} />
      <PresetListEditor title="🧾 GST %" hint="Khali chhodoge to 5 / 12 / 18 chalega" placeholder="Jaise 5" number suffix="%"
        items={presets.gstRates || []} onChange={(l) => savePresets({ ...presets, gstRates: l })} />
    </div>
  );
}

function DussehraRateEditor({ items, onSave, unavail = [], toggleAvail }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(items);
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [add, setAdd] = useState({ name: "", price: "", category: DUSSEHRA_CATEGORIES[0], portion: "" });
  useEffect(() => { if (!dirty) setDraft(items); }, [items, dirty]);

  const fld = { padding: "8px 10px", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 8, fontSize: 14, boxSizing: "border-box", fontFamily: "inherit" };
  const edit = (id, patch) => { setDraft((d) => d.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x))); setDirty(true); };
  const remove = (id) => { if (!window.confirm("Ye item hatana hai?")) return; setDraft((d) => d.filter((x) => x.id !== id)); setDirty(true); };
  const addItem = () => {
    const price = Number(add.price);
    if (!add.name.trim() || !Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0) { alert("⚠️ Naam aur sahi rate daalo"); return; }
    setDraft((d) => [...d, mi(uid("dz"), add.name.trim(), price, add.category, true, "", add.portion.trim(), false, true, "")]);
    setAdd({ ...add, name: "", price: "", portion: "" });
    setDirty(true);
  };
  const save = async () => {
    if (draft.some((x) => !String(x.name || "").trim() || !Number.isFinite(Number(x.price)) || Number(x.price) <= 0)) { alert("⚠️ Har item ka naam aur sahi rate hona chahiye"); return; }
    setSaving(true);
    await onSave(draft.map((x) => ({ ...x, price: Number(x.price) })));
    setSaving(false); setDirty(false);
  };
  const reset = () => { if (window.confirm("Saare Dussehra rates PDF wale original par wapas karne hain?")) { setDraft(DUSSEHRA_MENU); setDirty(true); } };

  if (!open) {
    return (
      <button onClick={() => setOpen(true)} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 14, marginBottom: 20, background: "#E65100", color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: 14, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 15, cursor: "pointer" }}>
        ✏️ Dussehra Rates Edit karo
      </button>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#fff", border: "1.5px solid #E65100", borderRadius: 14, padding: 16, marginBottom: 20 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 12, flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 8 }}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 16, color: "#E65100" }}>✏️ Dussehra Menu Rates</div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <button onClick={reset} style={{ ...fld, background: "transparent", color: COLORS.textLight, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>↩️ PDF wale par reset</button>
          <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} style={{ ...fld, background: COLORS.paper2, fontWeight: 700, cursor: "pointer" }}>✕ Band</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {DUSSEHRA_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => {
        const rows = draft.filter((x) => x.category === cat);
        if (rows.length === 0) return null;
        return (
          <div key={cat} style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 800, textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: 0.5, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 6 }}>{cat}</div>
            {rows.map((x) => (
              <div key={x.id} style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 6, opacity: unavail.includes(x.id) ? 0.55 : 1 }}>
                <input value={x.name} onChange={(e) => edit(x.id, { name: e.target.value })} style={{ ...fld, flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }} />
                {x.portion ? <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.textLight, fontWeight: 700, whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>{x.portion}</span> : null}
                <input type="number" inputMode="decimal" value={x.price} onChange={(e) => edit(x.id, { price: e.target.value })} style={{ ...fld, width: 80, fontWeight: 800, textAlign: "center" }} />
                <button onClick={() => toggleAvail && toggleAvail(x.id)} title="Hai / Khatam" style={{ ...fld, padding: "8px 8px", cursor: "pointer", background: unavail.includes(x.id) ? COLORS.error : COLORS.sageLight, color: unavail.includes(x.id) ? "#fff" : COLORS.sageDark, fontWeight: 800, border: "none" }}>{unavail.includes(x.id) ? "❌" : "✅"}</button>
                <button onClick={() => remove(x.id)} style={{ ...fld, padding: "8px 8px", cursor: "pointer", background: "transparent", color: COLORS.error, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.error}`, fontWeight: 800 }}>✕</button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        );
      })}

      <div style={{ background: COLORS.paper, borderRadius: 10, padding: 10, marginBottom: 12 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 800, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 6 }}>➕ Naya item</div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 6, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <input placeholder="Naam" value={add.name} onChange={(e) => setAdd({ ...add, name: e.target.value })} style={{ ...fld, flex: 2, minWidth: 140 }} />
          <input type="number" placeholder="Rate" value={add.price} onChange={(e) => setAdd({ ...add, price: e.target.value })} style={{ ...fld, width: 80 }} />
          <input placeholder="Half / Full" value={add.portion} onChange={(e) => setAdd({ ...add, portion: e.target.value })} style={{ ...fld, width: 90 }} />
          <select value={add.category} onChange={(e) => setAdd({ ...add, category: e.target.value })} style={{ ...fld, background: "#fff" }}>
            {DUSSEHRA_CATEGORIES.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
          </select>
          <button onClick={addItem} style={{ ...fld, background: COLORS.sage, color: "#fff", border: "none", fontWeight: 800, cursor: "pointer" }}>+ Add</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button onClick={save} disabled={!dirty || saving} style={{ width: "100%", padding: 14, border: "none", borderRadius: 12, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 15, cursor: dirty && !saving ? "pointer" : "not-allowed", background: dirty && !saving ? "#E65100" : COLORS.paper2, color: dirty && !saving ? "#fff" : COLORS.textLight }}>
        {saving ? "⏳ Saving..." : dirty ? "💾 Save Dussehra Menu" : "Koi badlav nahi"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function AdminView({ dussehraMenu = [], saveDussehraMenu, menu, unavail = [], toggleAvail, setMenuState, bookings, orders, markPaid, requestPinPrompt, inventory, addInventory, updateStock, deleteBooking, offersList, addOffer, removeOffer, loyaltyRules, setLoyaltyRules, loyaltyUsers, settings, setSettings, gallery, setGallery, categories, updateCategories, flashSaleItems, setFlashSaleItems, comboOffers, setComboOffers, savePromotions }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("overview");
  const [filterDate, setFilterDate] = useState(toLocalISODate(Date.now()));
  const [newInv, setNewInv] = useState({ name: "", stock: "", unit: "kg" });
  const [newOffer, setNewOffer] = useState({ title: "", desc: "" });
  const [newReward, setNewReward] = useState({ cost: "", item: "" });
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState(null);
  const [newItemName, setNewItemName] = useState("");
  const [newItemPrice, setNewItemPrice] = useState("");
  const [newItemCat, setNewItemCat] = useState((categories && categories[0]) || CATEGORIES[0]);
  const [newItemImage, setNewItemImage] = useState("");
  const [addMenuName, setAddMenuName] = useState("");
  const [addMenuPrice, setAddMenuPrice] = useState("");
  const [addMenuCat, setAddMenuCat] = useState((categories && categories[0]) || CATEGORIES[0]);
  const [addMenuVeg, setAddMenuVeg] = useState(true);
  const [addMenuDesc, setAddMenuDesc] = useState("");
  const [addMenuImage, setAddMenuImage] = useState("");
  const [newGalleryImg, setNewGalleryImg] = useState("");
  const [heroImgInput, setHeroImgInput] = useState(settings?.heroImage || "");
  const [isGeneratingPDF, setIsGeneratingPDF] = useState(false);
  const [newCategoryInput, setNewCategoryInput] = useState('');
  const [manageCat, setManageCat] = useState(null);
  const [manageSearch, setManageSearch] = useState("");
  const hiddenCats = settings?.hiddenCategories || [];
  // 🩹 ONE-SHOT MENU FIX — duplicates + naam + desc + photos (Wikipedia→Cloudinary)
  const [fixState, setFixState] = useState(null);
  const [fixReport, setFixReport] = useState(null);
  const [fixProgress, setFixProgress] = useState({ done: 0, total: 0, msg: "" });
  const CLOUD_NAME = "nnddxvcn";
  const UPLOAD_PRESET = "eatpark_uploads";
  const isOwnImage = (u) => /cloudinary\.com|pexels\.com/.test(u || "");
  const normalizeDishName = (s) => {
    let n = String(s || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    n = n.replace(/\bchilly\b/gi, "Chilli").replace(/\bchily\b/gi, "Chilli").replace(/\bchili\b/gi, "Chilli").replace(/\bmutter\b/gi, "Mutton");
    return n.split(" ").map(w => w ? w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1).toLowerCase() : "").join(" ");
  };
  const normalizeDesc = (s, name) => {
    let d = String(s || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!d || d.length < 6) return `${name || "Fresh dish"} — freshly prepared with quality ingredients.`;
    if (!/[.!?]$/.test(d)) d += ".";
    return d[0].toUpperCase() + d.slice(1);
  };
  const wikiImage = async (dishName) => {
    const clean = String(dishName || "").replace(/\(.*?\)/g, "").replace(/\b\d+\s*(pc|pcs|piece|pieces|kg|gm|ml|ltr|plate|eggs?)\b/gi, "").replace(/\b(half|full|quarter|regular|special|small|large|medium|dry|gravy|steam|fry|tandoori)\b/gi, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!clean) return "";
    try {
      const url = `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&generator=search&gsrsearch=${encodeURIComponent(clean + " food")}&gsrlimit=1&prop=pageimages&piprop=thumbnail|original&pithumbsize=600&format=json&origin=*`;
      const r = await fetch(url);
      if (!r.ok) return "";
      const d = await r.json();
      const first = Object.values(d?.query?.pages || {})[0];
      const img = first?.thumbnail?.source || first?.original?.source;
      if (img && /\.(jpe?g|png|webp)/i.test(img)) return img;
    } catch (e) {}
    return "";
  };
  const cloudinaryUpload = async (remoteUrl) => {
    try {
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", remoteUrl);
      fd.append("upload_preset", UPLOAD_PRESET);
      fd.append("folder", "eatpark_auto");
      const r = await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${CLOUD_NAME}/image/upload`, { method: "POST", body: fd });
      if (r.ok) { const d = await r.json(); if (d.secure_url) return d.secure_url; }
    } catch (e) {}
    try {
      const ir = await fetch(remoteUrl, { mode: "cors" });
      if (!ir.ok) return "";
      const blob = await ir.blob();
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", blob, "auto.jpg");
      fd.append("upload_preset", UPLOAD_PRESET);
      fd.append("folder", "eatpark_auto");
      const r = await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${CLOUD_NAME}/image/upload`, { method: "POST", body: fd });
      if (r.ok) { const d = await r.json(); return d.secure_url || ""; }
    } catch (e) {}
    return "";
  };
  const scanFix = () => {
    setFixState("scanning");
    try {
      const norm = (s) => String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      const richness = (m) => (isOwnImage(m.image) ? 20 : 0) + (m.image ? 5 : 0) + (m.desc ? 3 : 0) + (m.isBestseller ? 2 : 0) + ((m.alsoIn || []).length);
      const seen = {}, dupDeleteIds = new Set(), dupPairs = [];
      [...menu].sort((a, b) => richness(b) - richness(a)).forEach((m) => {
        const key = [norm(m.name), norm(m.portion), norm(m.category), Math.round(Number(m.price) || 0)].join("|");
        if (seen[key]) { dupDeleteIds.add(m.id); dupPairs.push({ keep: seen[key].name, del: m.name, price: m.price }); }
        else seen[key] = m;
      });
      const surviving = menu.filter(m => !dupDeleteIds.has(m.id));
      const nameFixes = [], descFixes = [];
      surviving.forEach(m => {
        const newName = normalizeDishName(m.name);
        if (newName && newName !== m.name) nameFixes.push({ id: m.id, from: m.name, to: newName });
        const newDesc = normalizeDesc(m.desc, newName || m.name);
        if (newDesc !== (m.desc || "")) descFixes.push({ id: m.id, from: m.desc || "(empty)", to: newDesc });
      });
      const imgTargets = surviving.filter(m => !isOwnImage(m.image)).map(m => ({ id: m.id, name: m.name, category: m.category }));
      setFixReport({ dupPairs, dupDeleteIds: [...dupDeleteIds], nameFixes, descFixes, imgTargets });
      setFixState("report");
    } catch (e) { alert("❌ Scan fail: " + e.message); setFixState(null); }
  };
  const applyFix = async () => {
    if (!fixReport) return;
    const { dupDeleteIds, nameFixes, descFixes, imgTargets } = fixReport;
    const totalSteps = dupDeleteIds.length + nameFixes.length + descFixes.length + imgTargets.length;
    if (!window.confirm(`Final fix:\n• ${dupDeleteIds.length} duplicates delete\n• ${nameFixes.length} naam fix\n• ${descFixes.length} description fix\n• ${imgTargets.length} photo fetch\n\nBackup pehle save hoga. Continue?`)) return;
    setFixState("applying");
    setFixProgress({ done: 0, total: totalSteps, msg: "Backup save..." });
    try {
      try { localStorage.setItem("menu_backup_" + Date.now(), JSON.stringify({ at: Date.now(), menu })); } catch (e) {}
      try { const a = document.createElement("a"); a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify({ at: Date.now(), menu }, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" })); a.download = `menu-backup-${Date.now()}.json`; a.click(); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 3000); } catch (e) {}
      const delSet = new Set(dupDeleteIds);
      const nameMap = Object.fromEntries(nameFixes.map(n => [n.id, n.to]));
      const descMap = Object.fromEntries(descFixes.map(n => [n.id, n.to]));
      let working = menu.filter(m => !delSet.has(m.id)).map(m => { let o = { ...m }; if (nameMap[m.id]) o.name = nameMap[m.id]; if (descMap[m.id]) o.desc = descMap[m.id]; return o; });
      let done = dupDeleteIds.length + nameFixes.length + descFixes.length;
      setFixProgress({ done, total: totalSteps, msg: "Photos fetch..." });
      for (let i = 0; i < imgTargets.length; i++) {
        const t = imgTargets[i];
        setFixProgress({ done: done + i, total: totalSteps, msg: `🖼️ ${i + 1}/${imgTargets.length}: ${t.name}` });
        try {
          const wikiUrl = await wikiImage(t.name);
          if (wikiUrl) { const cdnUrl = await cloudinaryUpload(wikiUrl); if (cdnUrl) working = working.map(m => m.id === t.id ? { ...m, image: cdnUrl } : m); }
        } catch (e) { console.warn("skip:", t.name); }
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));
      }
      setFixProgress({ done: totalSteps, total: totalSteps, msg: "Firestore save..." });
      await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "menu"), { items: working });
      setMenuState(working);
      setFixState("done");
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); alert("❌ Apply fail: " + e.message); setFixState("report"); }
  };
  const closeFix = () => { setFixState(null); setFixReport(null); setFixProgress({ done: 0, total: 0, msg: "" }); };


  const saveAppSetting = async (patch) => {
    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...patch }));
    try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "appSettings"), patch, { merge: true }); }
    catch (e) { console.error(e); alert("⚠️ Save nahi hua. Internet check karo."); }
  };
  const toggleVegMode = () => saveAppSetting({ vegMode: !settings?.vegMode });
  const toggleCategory = (c) => saveAppSetting({
    hiddenCategories: hiddenCats.includes(c) ? hiddenCats.filter((x) => x !== c) : [...hiddenCats, c]
  });
  const toggleItemInCategory = async (itemId, cat) => {
    const updatedMenu = menu.map((m) => {
      if (m.id !== itemId || m.category === cat) return m;
      const also = m.alsoIn || [];
      return { ...m, alsoIn: also.includes(cat) ? also.filter((x) => x !== cat) : [...also, cat] };
    });
    setMenuState(updatedMenu);
    try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "menu"), { items: updatedMenu }); }
    catch (e) { console.error(e); alert("⚠️ Menu save nahi hua. Internet check karo."); }
  };

  const [histOrders, setHistOrders] = useState([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (filterDate === toLocalISODate(Date.now())) { setHistOrders([]); return; }
    let off = false;
    const s = new Date(filterDate + "T00:00:00").getTime();
    getDocs(query(collection(db, "orders"), where("createdAt", ">=", s), where("createdAt", "<", s + 86400000)))
      .then((snap) => { if (!off) setHistOrders(snap.docs.map(normOrder)); })
      .catch((e) => console.error("history load:", e));
    return () => { off = true; };
  }, [filterDate]);
  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map();
    histOrders.forEach(o => m.set(o.id, o));
    orders.forEach(o => m.set(o.id, o));
    return Array.from(m.values()).filter(o => toLocalISODate(o.createdAt) === filterDate);
  }, [orders, histOrders, filterDate]);
  const revenue = filteredOrders.filter((o) => o.paid && o.status !== "cancelled").reduce((s, o) => s + getOrderTotal(o), 0);
  const _revenueOld = filteredOrders.filter((o) => o.paid).reduce((s, o) => s + o.items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.price * it.qty, 0) + (o.deliveryFee || 0) - (o.loyaltyDiscount || 0), 0);
  const avgOrderValue = (() => { const a = filteredOrders.filter(o => o.status !== "cancelled"); return a.length ? Math.round(a.reduce((s, o) => s + getOrderTotal(o), 0) / a.length) : 0; })();
  const _avgOld = filteredOrders.length > 0 ? Math.round(filteredOrders.reduce((s, o) => s + o.items.reduce((a, it) => a + it.price * it.qty, 0) - (o.loyaltyDiscount || 0), 0) / filteredOrders.length) : 0;

  const handleAddCategory = () => { if (!newCategoryInput.trim()) return; if (categories.some(c => c.toLowerCase() === newCategoryInput.trim().toLowerCase())) { alert("⚠️ Ye category pehle se hai"); return; } updateCategories([...categories, newCategoryInput.trim()]); setNewCategoryInput(''); };
  const handleMoveCategory = (index, direction) => { const ni = index + direction; if (ni < 0 || ni >= categories.length) return; const u = [...categories]; const [r] = u.splice(index, 1); u.splice(ni, 0, r); updateCategories(u); };
  const handleDeleteCategory = (index) => { if (menu.some(m => m.category === categories[index])) { alert("⚠️ Is category me items hain. Pehle unhe doosri category me daalo ya delete karo."); return; } if (window.confirm("Delete this category?")) { updateCategories(categories.filter((_, i) => i !== index)); } };

  const generatePDFReport = async () => {
    setIsGeneratingPDF(true);
    try {
      const loadScript = (src) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => { const s = document.createElement('script'); s.src = src; s.onload = resolve; s.onerror = reject; document.head.appendChild(s); });
      await loadScript('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/html2canvas@1.4.1/dist/html2canvas.min.js');
      await loadScript('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/jspdf@2.5.1/dist/jspdf.umd.min.js');
      const html2canvas = window.html2canvas;
      const jsPDF = window.jspdf.jsPDF;
      const reportElement = document.getElementById('report-content');
      if (!reportElement) { alert('Report not found'); setIsGeneratingPDF(false); return; }
      const canvas = await html2canvas(reportElement, { scale: 2 });
      const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      const pdf = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4');
      const imgWidth = 210;
      const imgHeight = (canvas.height * imgWidth) / canvas.width;
      pdf.addImage(imgData, 'PNG', 0, 0, imgWidth, imgHeight);
      pdf.save(`Sales_Report_${filterDate}.pdf`);
    } catch (e) { alert('⚠️ Could not generate PDF.'); } finally { setIsGeneratingPDF(false); }
  };

  const handleAddOffer = () => { if (newOffer.title) { addOffer({ id: uid("off"), title: newOffer.title, desc: newOffer.desc }); setNewOffer({ title: "", desc: "" }); } };
  const handleAddReward = () => { if (newReward.cost && newReward.item) { setLoyaltyRules({ ...loyaltyRules, rewards: [...loyaltyRules.rewards, { id: uid("rwd"), cost: Number(newReward.cost), item: newReward.item }] }); setNewReward({ cost: "", item: "" }); } };

  const handleSaveMenuItem = async () => {
    if (!editingItem) return;
    if (!String(newItemName).trim() || !(Number(newItemPrice) > 0)) { alert("⚠️ Naam aur sahi price daalo"); return; }
    const updatedMenu = menu.map(m => m.id === editingItem.id ? { ...m, name: newItemName, price: Number(newItemPrice), category: newItemCat, ...(newItemImage ? { image: newItemImage } : {}) } : m);
    setMenuState(updatedMenu);
    try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "menu"), { items: updatedMenu }); } catch (e) { alert("⚠️ Failed to save"); }
    setEditingItem(null); setNewItemImage("");
  };
  const [autoImgMsg, setAutoImgMsg] = useState("");
  const autoImagesAll = async () => {
    if (autoImgMsg) return;
    const isOwn = (u) => /cloudinary\.com|pexels\.com/.test(u || "");
    const targets = menu.filter((m) => !isOwn(m.image));
    if (!targets.length) { alert("Sab items ki photo pehle se set hai"); return; }
    if (!window.confirm(targets.length + " items ki photo auto lagani hain? (Cloudinary / apni upload ki hui photo nahi badlegi)")) return;
    const cache = {};
    const found = {};
    let calls = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) {
      const m = targets[i];
      const key = String(m.name || "").replace(/\(.*?\)/g, "").replace(/chilly/gi, "chilli").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!(key in cache)) {
        if (calls >= 150) { alert("150 request ho gayi (Pexels limit 200/ghanta). Thodi der baad dobara dabao, baaki items ho jayenge."); break; }
        calls++;
        setAutoImgMsg("⏳ " + (i + 1) + "/" + targets.length + " — " + m.name);
        try { const r = await fetch("/api/image?q=" + encodeURIComponent(key)); const d = await r.json(); cache[key] = d.url || ""; } catch (e) { cache[key] = ""; }
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
      }
      if (cache[key]) found[m.id] = cache[key];
    }
    const n = Object.keys(found).length;
    if (n > 0) {
      const updated = menu.map((m) => (found[m.id] ? { ...m, image: found[m.id] } : m));
      setMenuState(updated);
      try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "menu"), { items: updated }); alert("✅ " + n + " items ki photo lag gayi"); }
      catch (e) { alert("⚠️ Save nahi hua: " + e.message); }
    }
    setAutoImgMsg("");
  };
  const handleDeleteMenuItem = async (id) => { if (window.confirm("Delete this item?")) { const u = menu.filter(m => m.id !== id); setMenuState(u); try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "menu"), { items: u }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); alert("⚠️ Menu save nahi hua. Internet check karo."); } } };
  const handleAddNewDish = async () => {
    if (!addMenuName.trim() || !addMenuPrice) { alert("Enter name and price"); return; }
    const newDish = mi(uid("m"), addMenuName.trim(), Number(addMenuPrice), addMenuCat, addMenuVeg, addMenuDesc.trim(), "", false, true, addMenuImage.trim());
    const u = [newDish, ...menu];
    setMenuState(u);
    try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "menu"), { items: u }); } catch (e) { }
    setAddMenuName(""); setAddMenuPrice(""); setAddMenuDesc(""); setAddMenuImage("");
    alert("✅ Dish added!");
  };
  const handleAddGalleryPhoto = async () => { if (!newGalleryImg.trim()) return; const u = [...gallery, newGalleryImg.trim()]; setGallery(u); try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "gallery"), { images: u }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); alert("⚠️ Gallery save nahi hui. Internet check karo."); } setNewGalleryImg(""); };
  const handleDeleteGalleryPhoto = async (index) => { const u = gallery.filter((_, i) => i !== index); setGallery(u); try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "gallery"), { images: u }); } catch (e) { } };
  const handleSaveHeroImage = async () => { if (!heroImgInput.trim()) return; const n = { ...settings, heroImage: heroImgInput.trim() }; setSettings(n); try { await setDoc(doc(db, "settings", "appSettings"), { heroImage: heroImgInput.trim() }, { merge: true }); alert("✅ Saved!"); } catch (e) { alert("⚠️ Save nahi hua: " + e.message); } };
  const handleExportCSV = () => {
    const rows = [["Time", "Type", "Items", "Total", "Paid"]];
    filteredOrders.forEach(o => { const t = getOrderTotal(o); rows.push([new Date(o.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN'), o.orderType === "parcel" ? "Parcel" : `Table ${o.table}`, o.items.map(it => `${it.qty}x ${it.name}`).join("; "), t, o.paid ? "Yes" : "No"]); });
    const csv = rows.map(r => r.map(c => `"${String(HX.csvCell(c)).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(",")).join("\n");
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = 'data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent("\uFEFF" + csv);
    link.download = `orders_${filterDate}.csv`; link.click();
  };

  const inputStyle = { padding: "12px 16px", border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 12, fontSize: 16, width: "100%", boxSizing: "border-box" };
  const primaryBtn = { background: COLORS.copper, color: "#fff", border: "none", borderRadius: 14, padding: "13px 20px", fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 14, cursor: "pointer" };
  const th = { padding: "12px 14px", borderBottom: `2px solid ${COLORS.line}` };
  const td = { padding: "12px 14px", borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}` };

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: "26px 20px 60px", maxWidth: 1100, margin: "0 auto" }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 28, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <div style={{ fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif", fontSize: 32, fontWeight: 800 }}>📊 Admin Dashboard <ViewModeToggle /></div>
        <button onClick={() => requestPinPrompt("customer")} style={{ background: COLORS.paper2, border: "none", padding: "10px 20px", borderRadius: 12, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 700 }}>← Exit</button>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, marginBottom: 28, borderBottom: `2px solid ${COLORS.line}`, overflowX: "auto" }}>
        {["overview", "menu", "tables", "presets", "gallery", "settings", "offers", "loyalty", "orders", "bookings", "promotions"].map((t) => (<button key={t} onClick={() => setTab(t)} style={{ background: "none", border: "none", padding: "14px 20px", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 15, textTransform: "capitalize", color: tab === t ?               <div key={cat} style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', background: '#fff', padding: '10px 16px', borderRadius: 10, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 700, opacity: hiddenCats.includes(cat) ? 0.5 : 1 }}>{index + 1}. {cat}</span>
                  <button onClick={() => toggleCategory(cat)} style={{ padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: 8, border: 'none', fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 12, cursor: 'pointer', background: hiddenCats.includes(cat) ? COLORS.error : COLORS.sage, color: '#fff' }}>
                    {hiddenCats.includes(cat) ? '❌ OFF' : '✅ ON'}
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => { setManageCat(cat); setManageSearch(""); }} style={{ padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: 8, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.info}`, background: 'transparent', color: COLORS.info, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 12, cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                    📋 Items ({menu.filter((m) => m.category === cat || (m.alsoIn || []).includes(cat)).length})
                  </button>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}>
                  <button onClick={() => handleMoveCategory(index, -1)} disabled={index === 0} style={{ padding: '4px 10px', opacity: index === 0 ? 0.4 : 1 }}>⬆️</button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleMoveCategory(index, 1)} disabled={index === categories.length - 1} style={{ padding: '4px 10px', opacity: index === categories.length - 1 ? 0.4 : 1 }}>⬇️</button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleDeleteCategory(index)} style={{ padding: '4px 10px', color: COLORS.error, background: 'transparent', border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.error}`, borderRadius: 6, fontWeight: 700 }}>✕</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div style={{ background: COLORS.paper, padding: 24, borderRadius: 16, marginBottom: 30 }}>
            <h3 style={{ marginTop: 0, marginBottom: 16 }}>➕ Add Dish</h3>
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <input type="text" placeholder="Dish Name *" value={addMenuName} onChange={e => setAddMenuName(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} />
              <input type="number" placeholder="Price *" value={addMenuPrice} onChange={e => setAddMenuPrice(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} />
              <select value={addMenuCat} onChange={e => setAddMenuCat(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle}>{categories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}</select>
              <select value={addMenuVeg} onChange={e => setAddMenuVeg(e.target.value === 'true')} style={inputStyle}><option value="true">🟢 Veg</option><option value="false">🔴 Non-Veg</option></select>
            </div>
            <input type="url" placeholder="Image URL (optional)" value={addMenuImage} onChange={e => setAddMenuImage(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, marginBottom: 12 }} />
            <textarea placeholder="Description" value={addMenuDesc} onChange={e => setAddMenuDesc(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, marginBottom: 16, resize: 'none' }} rows={2} />
            <button onClick={handleAddNewDish} style={primaryBtn}>+ Add Dish</button>
          </div>

          <h3 style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>Existing Menu</h3>

            <div key={item.id} style={{ background: '#fff', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, padding: 16, borderRadius: 14, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <img src={item.image} alt="" style={{ width: 50, height: 50, objectFit: 'cover', borderRadius: 10 }} onError={(e) => { e.target.onerror = null; e.target.src = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1546069901-ba9599a7e63c?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80"; }} />
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}><div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 16 }}>{item.name} <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: COLORS.textLight }}>({item.category})</span></div><div style={{ color: COLORS.copper, fontWeight: 800 }}>{inr(item.price)}</div></div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
                <button onClick={() => { setEditingItem(item); setNewItemName(item.name); setNewItemPrice(item.price); setNewItemCat(item.category); setNewItemImage(item.image); }} style={{ background: COLORS.paper2, border: 'none', padding: '8px 14px', borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 700, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Edit</button>
                <button onClick={() => toggleAvail && toggleAvail(item.id)} style={{ background: unavail.includes(item.id) ? COLORS.error : COLORS.sageLight, color: unavail.includes(item.id) ? '#fff' : COLORS.sageDark, border: 'none', padding: '8px 14px', borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 800, cursor: 'pointer' }}>{unavail.includes(item.id) ? '❌ Khatam' : '✅ Hai'}</button>
                <button onClick={() => handleDeleteMenuItem(item.id)} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '8px 14px', borderRadius: 8, fontWeight: 700, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Delete</button>
              </div>
            </div>

          {manageCat && (
            <div style={{ position: 'fixed', inset: 0, background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', zIndex: 99 }} onClick={() => setManageCat(null)}>
              <div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} style={{ background: '#fff', padding: 20, borderRadius: 20, width: '92%', maxWidth: 480, maxHeight: '85vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 4 }}>
                  <h3 style={{ margin: 0 }}>📋 {manageCat}</h3>
                  <button onClick={() => setManageCat(null)} style={{ background: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.05)', border: 'none', borderRadius: '50%', width: 32, height: 32, cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 16 }}>✕</button>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: COLORS.textLight, marginBottom: 10 }}>Tick karo = item is category me bhi dikhega (original category me bhi rahega)</div>
                <input placeholder="🔍 Menu me search..." value={manageSearch} onChange={(e) => setManageSearch(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, marginBottom: 10, padding: '10px 14px', fontSize: 14 }} />
                <div style={{ overflowY: 'auto', flex: 1, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, borderRadius: 12 }}>

                    .filter((m) => !manageSearch.trim() || m.name.toLowerCase().includes(manageSearch.toLowerCase()))
                    .map((m) => {
                      const own = m.category === manageCat;
                      const checked = own || (m.alsoIn || []).includes(manageCat);
                      return (
                        <label key={m.id} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, padding: '10px 12px', borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, cursor: own ? 'default' : 'pointer', opacity: own ? 0.6 : 1 }}>
                          <input type="checkbox" checked={checked} disabled={own} onChange={() => toggleItemInCategory(m.id, manageCat)} style={{ width: 18, height: 18 }} />
                          <VegDot veg={m.veg} />
                          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700 }}>{m.name}{m.portion ? ` (${m.portion})` : ""}</div>
                            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.textLight }}>{m.category} · ₹{m.price}{own ? " · yahi original category" : ""}</div>
                          </div>
                        </label>
                      );
                    })}

          {editingItem && (

                <h3 style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Edit Item</h3>
                <input type="text" value={newItemName} onChange={e => setNewItemName(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, marginBottom: 12 }} />
                <input type="number" value={newItemPrice} onChange={e => setNewItemPrice(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, marginBottom: 12 }} />
                <select value={newItemCat} onChange={e => setNewItemCat(e.target.value)} style={{ ...inputStyle, marginBottom: 12 }}>{categories.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}</select>
<div style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
  <ImageUploadButton 
    currentImage={newItemImage} 
    onUploaded={setNewItemImage}
    label="📸 Upload New Photo"
  />
</div>                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12 }}>
                  <button onClick={() => setEditingItem(null)} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 12, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, background: 'transparent', borderRadius: 10, fontWeight: 700, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Cancel</button>
                  <button onClick={handleSaveMenuItem} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 12, background: COLORS.copper, color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 10, fontWeight: 800, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Save</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {tab === "loyalty" && (
        <>
          <div style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #fdfbfb, #ebedee)', padding: 24, borderRadius: 16, marginBottom: 24, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.gold}` }}>
            <div style={{ fontSize: 20, marginBottom: 16, fontWeight: 800 }}>🪙 EatCoin Settings</div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 20 }}>

              <input type="text" placeholder="Free item" value={newReward.item} onChange={e => setNewReward({ ...newReward, item: e.target.value })} style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 2 }} />
              <button onClick={handleAddReward} style={{ ...primaryBtn, background: COLORS.gold, color: COLORS.ink }}>+ Add</button>
            </div>
          </div>

            <div key={r.id} style={{ background: '#fff', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, padding: 20, borderRadius: 14, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <div><div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 18 }}>{r.item}</div><div style={{ color: COLORS.gold, fontWeight: 800 }}>{r.cost} Coins</div></div>
              <button onClick={() => setLoyaltyRules({ ...loyaltyRules, rewards: loyaltyRules.rewards.filter(rw => rw.id !== r.id) })} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 800 }}>Remove</button>
            </div>
          ))}

          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", background: "#fff", borderRadius: 12, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
            <thead><tr style={{ background: COLORS.paper }}><th style={th}>Name</th><th style={th}>Phone</th><th style={th}>Coins</th><th style={th}>Tier</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>{[...loyaltyUsers].sort((a, b) => b.coins - a.coins).map((u, i) => (<tr key={i}><td style={td}>{u.name}</td><td style={td}>{u.phone}</td><td style={{ ...td, fontWeight: 800, color: COLORS.sageDark }}>{u.coins}</td><td style={td}>{getLoyaltyTier(u.coins).name}</td></tr>))}</tbody>
          </table>

      {tab === "offers" && (
        <>
          <div style={{ background: COLORS.paper, padding: 24, borderRadius: 16, marginBottom: 24 }}>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12 }}>
              <input type="text" placeholder="Title" value={newOffer.title} onChange={e => setNewOffer({ ...newOffer, title: e.target.value })} style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 1 }} />
              <input type="text" placeholder="Description" value={newOffer.desc} onChange={e => setNewOffer({ ...newOffer, desc: e.target.value })} style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 2 }} />
              <button onClick={handleAddOffer} style={primaryBtn}>+ Add</button>
            </div>
          </div>

            <div key={off.id} style={{ background: '#fff', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, padding: 20, borderRadius: 14, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <div><div style={{ fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 18 }}>{off.title}</div><div style={{ color: COLORS.textLight, fontWeight: 600 }}>{off.desc}</div></div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
                <button onClick={() => { const nt = prompt("Title:", off.title); if (nt !== null) { const nd = prompt("Desc:", off.desc); if (nd !== null) { addOffer({ ...off, title: nt.trim(), desc: nd.trim() }); } } }} style={{ background: COLORS.copperLight, border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.copper}`, color: COLORS.copperDark, padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 700 }}>✏️</button>
                <button onClick={() => removeOffer(off.id)} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '8px 16px', borderRadius: 10, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 800 }}>Delete</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </>
      )}

      

      {tab === "orders" && (
        <div style={{ overflowX: "auto", borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}` }}>
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 14, background: "#fff" }}>
            <thead><tr style={{ textAlign: "left", background: COLORS.paper }}><th style={th}>Time</th><th style={th}>Table</th><th style={th}>Items</th><th style={th}>Total</th><th style={th}>Payment</th><th style={th}>Action</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {[...filteredOrders].sort((a, b) => b.createdAt - a.createdAt).map((o) => (
                <tr key={o.id} style={{ opacity: o.status === "cancelled" ? 0.5 : 1 }}>
                  <td style={{ ...td, color: COLORS.textLight, fontWeight: 600 }}>{new Date(o.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}</td>
                  <td style={td}>{o.orderType === "parcel" ? <Badge color={COLORS.copper}>Parcel</Badge> : <strong>T-{o.table}</strong>}{o.status === "cancelled" && <span style={{ color: COLORS.error, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 700, display: 'block' }}>❌ Cancelled</span>}</td>
                  <td style={td}>{o.items.map((it) => `${it.qty}×${it.name}`).join(", ")}</td>
                  <td style={{ ...td, fontWeight: 700 }}>{inr(getOrderTotal(o))}</td>
                  <td style={td}>{o.payment || "cash"}<span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: o.paid ? COLORS.sage : COLORS.textLight, display: 'block' }}>{o.paid ? "✅" : "⏳"}</span></td>
                  <td style={td}><button onClick={() => markPaid(o.id, o.paid && !o.coinsClaimed ? true : !o.paid)} style={{ border: `1.5px solid ${o.paid ? COLORS.sage : COLORS.line}`, background: o.paid ? COLORS.sage : "transparent", color: o.paid ? "#fff" : COLORS.ink, borderRadius: 10, padding: "6px 14px", fontSize: 13, cursor: "pointer", fontWeight: 700 }}>{o.paid ? (o.coinsClaimed ? "✓ Paid" : "🪙 Award coins") : "Mark paid"}</button></td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

            {tab === "bookings" && (
        <div style={{ overflowX: "auto", borderRadius: 16, border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}` }}>
          <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 14, background: "#fff" }}>
            <thead><tr style={{ background: COLORS.paper }}><th style={th}>Type</th><th style={th}>Details</th><th style={th}>Date</th><th style={th}>Action</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {bookings.map(b => (
                <tr key={b.id}>
                  <td style={td}>{b.type === "party" ? "🎉 Party" : "🍽️ Table"}</td>
                  <td style={td}><strong>{b.name}</strong><br />{b.phone}<br />{b.guests} Guests</td>
                  <td style={td}>{b.date} at {b.time}</td>
                  <td style={td}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                      <a 
                        href={`https://wa.me/91${b.phone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
                          `Hello ${b.name}! 🍽️\n\nAapki ${b.type === "party" ? "Party" : "Table"} booking confirm ho gayi hai.\n\n📅 Date: ${b.date}\n⏰ Time: ${b.time}\n👥 Guests: ${b.guests}\n\nEat & Park mein aapka swagat hai! 🎉`
                        )}`}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noopener noreferrer"
                        style={{ 
                          background: '#25D366', color: '#fff', 
                          padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, 
                          textDecoration: 'none', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 12,
                          display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4
                        }}
                      >
                        ✅ Confirm
                      </a>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => deleteBooking(b.id)} 
                        style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1.5px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 700 }}
                      >
                        Delete
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === "promotions" && (
        <>
          <h3 style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>⚡ Flash Sale</h3>
          {flashSaleItems.map((item, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} style={{ background: '#fff', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <select value={item.id}
                  onChange={e => { const m = menu.find(x => x.id === e.target.value); if (m) setFlashSaleItems(patchAt(flashSaleItems, idx, { id: m.id, name: m.name, price: m.price })); }}
                  style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 2, minWidth: 200 }}>
                  {!menu.some(m => m.id === item.id) && <option value={item.id}>⚠️ {item.name} (menu mein nahi)</option>}
                  {menu.map(m => <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.name}{m.portion ? ` (${m.portion})` : ""} — ₹{m.price}</option>)}
                </select>
                <input type="number" placeholder="Sale ₹" value={item.discountPrice} onChange={e => setFlashSaleItems(patchAt(flashSaleItems, idx, { discountPrice: Number(e.target.value) }))} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 100 }} />
                <input type="number" placeholder="Stock" value={item.stock} onChange={e => setFlashSaleItems(patchAt(flashSaleItems, idx, { stock: Number(e.target.value) }))} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 90 }} />
                <button onClick={() => setFlashSaleItems(patchAt(flashSaleItems, idx, { active: !item.active }))} style={{ background: item.active ? COLORS.sage : COLORS.error, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 700 }}>{item.active ? '✅ Active' : '❌ Inactive'}</button>
                <button onClick={() => { if (window.confirm('Delete?')) setFlashSaleItems(flashSaleItems.filter((_, i) => i !== idx)); }} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Delete</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          <button onClick={() => {
            const m = menu[0]; if (!m) return;
            setFlashSaleItems([...flashSaleItems, { id: m.id, name: m.name, price: m.price, discountPrice: Math.round(m.price * 0.8), stock: 10, active: true }]);
          }} style={{ ...primaryBtn, background: COLORS.sage, marginBottom: 30 }}>+ Add Flash Item</button>

          {comboOffers.map((combo, idx) => {
            const broken = !combo.items?.every(it => menu.some(m => m.id === it.id));
            return (
              <div key={idx} style={{ background: '#fff', border: `1px solid ${broken ? COLORS.error : COLORS.line}`, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
                {broken && <div style={{ color: COLORS.error, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>⚠️ Is combo ke kuch items menu mein nahi hain — customers ko dikhega nahi. Delete karke naya banao.</div>}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <input value={combo.name} onChange={e => setComboOffers(patchAt(comboOffers, idx, { name: e.target.value }))} style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 2 }} />
                  <input type="number" value={combo.discount} onChange={e => { const d = Number(e.target.value); setComboOffers(patchAt(comboOffers, idx, { discount: d, finalPrice: Math.round(combo.totalPrice * (1 - d / 100)) })); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 100 }} />
                  <input value={combo.image} onChange={e => setComboOffers(patchAt(comboOffers, idx, { image: e.target.value }))} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 80 }} />
                  <button onClick={() => setComboOffers(patchAt(comboOffers, idx, { active: !combo.active }))} style={{ background: combo.active ? COLORS.sage : COLORS.error, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 700 }}>{combo.active ? '✅' : '❌'}</button>
                  <button onClick={() => { if (window.confirm('Delete?')) setComboOffers(comboOffers.filter((_, i) => i !== idx)); }} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Delete</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          <button onClick={() => {
            const picks = menu.slice(0, 2); if (picks.length === 0) return;
            const total = picks.reduce((s, m) => s + m.price, 0);
            setComboOffers([...comboOffers, {
              id: 'combo' + Date.now(), name: 'New Combo',
              items: picks.map(m => ({ id: m.id, name: m.name, price: m.price, quantity: 1 })),
              totalPrice: total, discount: 10, finalPrice: Math.round(total * 0.9), image: '🍽️', active: true
            }]);
          }} style={{ ...primaryBtn, background: COLORS.gold, marginBottom: 20 }}>+ Add Combo</button>
          <button onClick={savePromotions} style={{ ...primaryBtn, width: '100%' }}>💾 Save All</button>
        </>
      )}
      {false /* legacy promotions UI removed */ && (
        <>
          <h3 style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>⚡ Flash Sale</h3>
          {flashSaleItems.map((item, idx) => (
            <div key={item.id} style={{ background: '#fff', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <input value={item.name} onChange={e => { const u = [...flashSaleItems]; u[idx].name = e.target.value; setFlashSaleItems(u); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 2 }} />
                <input type="number" value={item.discountPrice} onChange={e => { const u = [...flashSaleItems]; u[idx].discountPrice = Number(e.target.value); setFlashSaleItems(u); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 100 }} />
                <input type="number" value={item.stock} onChange={e => { const u = [...flashSaleItems]; u[idx].stock = Number(e.target.value); setFlashSaleItems(u); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 80 }} />
                <button onClick={() => { const u = [...flashSaleItems]; u[idx].active = !u[idx].active; setFlashSaleItems(u); }} style={{ background: item.active ? COLORS.sage : COLORS.error, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 700 }}>{item.active ? '✅ Active' : '❌ Inactive'}</button>
                <button onClick={() => { if (window.confirm('Delete?')) setFlashSaleItems(flashSaleItems.filter((_, i) => i !== idx)); }} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Delete</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          <button onClick={() => setFlashSaleItems([...flashSaleItems, { id: 'flash' + Date.now(), name: 'New', price: 0, discountPrice: 0, stock: 0, active: true }])} style={{ ...primaryBtn, background: COLORS.sage, marginBottom: 30 }}>+ Add Flash Item</button>

          <h3 style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>🎯 Combo Offers</h3>
          {comboOffers.map((combo, idx) => (
            <div key={combo.id} style={{ background: '#fff', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.line}`, padding: 16, borderRadius: 12, marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <input value={combo.name} onChange={e => { const u = [...comboOffers]; u[idx].name = e.target.value; setComboOffers(u); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, flex: 2 }} />
                <input type="number" value={combo.discount} onChange={e => { const u = [...comboOffers]; u[idx].discount = Number(e.target.value); u[idx].finalPrice = Math.round(u[idx].totalPrice * (1 - u[idx].discount / 100)); setComboOffers(u); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 100 }} />
                <input value={combo.image} onChange={e => { const u = [...comboOffers]; u[idx].image = e.target.value; setComboOffers(u); }} style={{ ...inputStyle, width: 80 }} />
                <button onClick={() => { const u = [...comboOffers]; u[idx].active = !u[idx].active; setComboOffers(u); }} style={{ background: combo.active ? COLORS.sage : COLORS.error, color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 700 }}>{combo.active ? '✅' : '❌'}</button>
                <button onClick={() => { if (window.confirm('Delete?')) setComboOffers(comboOffers.filter((_, i) => i !== idx)); }} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: `1px solid ${COLORS.rust}`, color: COLORS.rust, padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: 8, cursor: 'pointer' }}>Delete</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          <button onClick={() => setComboOffers([...comboOffers, { id: 'combo' + Date.now(), name: 'New Combo', items: [{ id: 'f2', name: 'Pizza', price: 280, quantity: 1 }], totalPrice: 280, discount: 10, finalPrice: 252, image: '🍽️', active: true }])} style={{ ...primaryBtn, background: COLORS.gold, marginBottom: 20 }}>+ Add Combo</button>
          <button onClick={savePromotions} style={{ ...primaryBtn, width: '100%' }}>💾 Save All</button>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ==
// 20. ProgressRing & OrderTimer
// ==
const ProgressRing = memo(({ progress, size = 60, strokeWidth = 3 }) => {
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * ((size - strokeWidth) / 2);
  const offset = circumference - (progress / 100) * circumference;
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size}>
      <g transform={`rotate(-90 ${size / 2} ${size / 2})`}>
        <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={(size - strokeWidth) / 2}
          fill="none" stroke={COLORS.line} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} />
        <circle cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={(size - strokeWidth) / 2}
          fill="none" stroke={COLORS.sage} strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
          strokeDasharray={circumference} strokeDashoffset={offset}
          style={{ transition: 'stroke-dashoffset 0.5s ease' }} />
      </g>
      <text x="50%" y="50%" textAnchor="middle" dy="0.35em"
        fontSize="16" fontWeight="700" fill={COLORS.sage}>
        {progress}%
      </text>
    </svg>
  );
});

const OrderTimer = memo(({ createdAt, servedAt, status, estimatedTime }) => {
  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'served' || status === 'delivered' || status === 'completed') {
      if (servedAt) setElapsed(Math.floor((servedAt - createdAt) / 1000));
      return;
    }
    if (status === 'cancelled') return;
    const t = setInterval(() => setElapsed(Math.floor((Date.now() - createdAt) / 1000)), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [createdAt, servedAt, status]);
  const minutes = Math.floor(elapsed / 60); const seconds = elapsed % 60; const isOvertime = elapsed > (estimatedTime * 60) && status !== 'served' && status !== 'delivered' && status !== 'completed';
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, padding: '8px 12px', background: isOvertime ? 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)' : COLORS.sageLight, borderRadius: 10, borderLeft: `3px solid ${isOvertime ? COLORS.error : COLORS.sage}` }}>
      <span style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 800, color: isOvertime ? COLORS.error : COLORS.sageDark }}>⏱️ {minutes}:{seconds.toString().padStart(2, '0')}</span>
      <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: COLORS.textLight, fontWeight: 600 }}>/ {estimatedTime}m</span>
    </div>
  );
});
